+## 滋賀の実更新メール検証完了（追記・最新）
+
+**滋賀：実更新メールの本文生成互換性PASS（A）。実旧DBによる更新判定Bは未検証。** 下部にある滋賀接続切替待ち・未検証の記述は広島完了時点の履歴であり、この追記で更新する。
+
+ユーザーによるGmail接続切替後、接続アカウントが滋賀控え用であることを確認。最初の検索はGmailのRATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDEDで失敗したため繰り返さず停止し、ユーザーの継続指示後の再試行で成功。本文検索 `"滋賀県警アラート（更新）"` は2通、next_page_tokenなし。件名に更新を要求せず、全結果の生MIMEを読み取り専用で取得した。
+
+| 滋賀不審者の集計 | 件数 |
+|---|---:|
+| 実更新メール発見・原本取得 | 2 |
+| 現在公開JSONと同ID | 2 |
+| 公開現在値＋仮旧値からの更新本文全バイト一致 | 2 |
+| 当時のメール記載値＋仮旧値からの更新本文全バイト一致 | 2 |
+| CRLF・本文末尾一致 | 2 |
+| MIME Subjectヘッダーバイト一致 | 2 |
+| Supabase既存recordsに同IDあり | 2 |
+| 本文不一致・対象外・ID欠落・読取失敗 | 0 |
+
+既存collectorで滋賀708件を取得。2通ともtext/plain・UTF-8・8bitで、raw MIMEの8bit本文バイトを比較した。更新マークは2通ともresolutionのみにあり、他の項目は現在値と同じ仮旧値とした。既存generateMailにより更新見出し、見出し末尾の●、全13項目、resolutionの●更新、注釈、市町名、Google Maps URL、全角空白、末尾CRLFまで完全一致。新たな本文生成処理は実装していない。旧DBや本当の旧resolution値の復元・変更検出の独立検証ではない。SupabaseはID存在の照合のみでpayloadの全バイト一致は主張しない。
+
+関連8ファイル208テストを再実行し全件成功。今回追加は文書のみで検証コードの変更なし。直前の追加コードstrict型チェック成功・全体型診断は既存15件で変更前後同一、という切り分け結果を維持する。既存新規97通と広島実更新591通の検証成果を保持。
+
+| 更新メール全体 | 発見 | 公開同ID | 公開現在値から完全一致 | 当時の記載値から完全一致 | 本文不一致 |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
+| 広島犯罪 | 530 | 0 | 0 | 530 | 0 |
+| 広島不審者 | 61 | 24 | 24 | 61 | 0 |
+| 滋賀不審者 | 2 | 2 | 2 | 2 | 0 |
+| 合計 | 593 | 26 | 26 | 593 | 0 |
+
+広島の公開ID欠落567通は引き続き公開実データ照合の対象外で、当時メール記載値によるAの証拠のみ。全593通の生成互換性AはPASSだが、B、配送From/envelope、センター最終受信・解析互換、未対応MIME入力、送信キューと切替境界は未認定。実メール送信、SMTP接続、本番Edge/DB変更、18時cron有効化、旧Windows停止、PRマージは今回もすべて未実施。
+
+---
+
+# 実更新メールの本文生成互換性（A）の検証
+
+既存Draft PR #155 / Issue #154の継続。既存collect.ts、generateMail、generateEmlを使用し、本文生成器・本番Edge・DB・cronは変更しない。個別本文、ID、事件詳細、住所、座標、認証値、生MIMEはGitへ保存しない。
+
+## 判定
+
+**広島：実更新メールの本文生成互換性PASS（A）。実旧DBによる更新判定・変更項目検出（B）は未検証。**
+
+Gmail本文検索 `"広島県警アラート（更新）"` を日付制限なしで実行し、100件×5ページ＋91件の計6ページ591通、次ページなしまで確認。件名に更新を要求していない。include_raw_mimeで全591通のRFC822 base64urlを読み取り、全通text/plain・UTF-8・8bitを確認した。8bit本文の元バイトとUTF-8再生成本文を比較し、通常のbody表示をバイト比較に流用していない。全通が実更新通知で、対象外メール0通、取得/パース失敗0通。
+
+| 項目（メール通数） | 広島犯罪 | 広島不審者 | 合計 |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|
+| 発見・原本取得 | 530 | 61 | 591 |
+| 現在公開JSONと同じID | 0 | 24 | 24 |
+| 同ID・公開現在値から仮旧値を構成し本文全バイト一致 | 0 | 24 | 24 |
+| 同IDが公開JSONにない | 530 | 37 | 567 |
+| 実メール記載値から仮旧値を構成し本文全バイト一致 | 530 | 61 | 591 |
+| CRLF・末尾を含む本文不一致 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
+| 件名のMIMEヘッダーバイト一致 | 530 | 61 | 591 |
+| Supabase recordsにも同IDあり | 0 | 24 | 24 |
+
+不審者は59の異なるIDに対する61通（同IDの複数更新も個別に検証）。犯罪は530の異なるID。2026年の検索結果26通はすべて不審者（うち公開ID一致21通）。残りは2025年の不審者35通・犯罪530通。検索範囲を2026-11-04だけに限定した前回の未発見結果とは区別する。
+
+**567通は公開実データとの完全一致認定の対象外だが、当時の実メールに記録された値による生成互換性Aの検証対象に含めた。** 公開現在値による一致24通と、当時のメール記載値による一致591通を同じ証拠として扱わない。公開IDがないことを本文不一致として数えない。滋賀の実更新メールは接続先切替待ちで今回0通・未検証。
+
+## 仮旧値と検証範囲
+
+実原本の各フィールド行と末尾の「●更新」を解析し、印付き項目だけ現在値と必ず異なる合成文字列、印なしは同じ値を入れたpreviousを生成。既存generateMail()へ渡し、**更新マークを削除して比較せず**再生成した更新本文そのものを比較した。更新見出し、末尾の●、全項目・順序・注釈・全角空白・市町名・署名・URL・本文末尾が比較対象。印の位置異常、未認識印、項目欠落・重複は拒否する。
+
+公開同IDがある24通はcollectorの現在値と市町/署参照を使用しても全バイト一致。残りはメール本文の当時値と参照名を抽出。広島不審者resolutionは表示ラベルから描画等価のコードを構成する。空表示から元のnull/空文字/その他コードは特定できないため、復元したと主張しない。
+
+Aは観測した変更項目の組み合わせを同じ形式で描画できることの証明であり、観測マーク自体の正当性を独立に検証するものではない。更新前SQL Server、当時の公開JSON、当時の比較状態はない。**B、新規/更新の選別、座標のみ変更時の送信判定、送信対象件数の再現は未認定**。犯罪メールの更新印は緯度493通・経度75通（重複あり）に観測されたが、その存在からplan()の送信判定を変更しない。
+
+## 公開から消えたIDの調査
+
+今回のcollectorは広島6,089件（不審者454・犯罪5,635）を正常取得。IDがない567通はSupabaseの既存recordsにも同IDがなかった。DBはID存在数のみ照合しpayloadの完全一致は今回の認定対象外。
+
+不審者の公開発生日期間は2025-11-05〜2026-11-02、欠落37通の当時発生日は2025-03-02〜2025-10-27。期間外の古い情報が公開対象から外れた可能性と整合するが、削除理由の確証はない。
+
+犯罪の欠落530通の発生日は2025-01-29〜2025-08-18で、現在犯罪JSONの発生日期間（2020-11-01〜2026-11-01）内にあるため、単純な期間外では説明できない。旧IDを部分文字列として含む現在IDは8通分で見つかったが、発生開始/終了日時・市コード・町域・カテゴリの同時一致は530通とも0件。ID変更・再掲・削除・仕様変更のいずれかは確定できず、類似IDを同じ事案と扱わない。旧JSONや提供元の履歴がないため理由は未確定のまま記録する。
+
+## 実装・再現
+
+既存scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail.tsを拡張し、scripts/jaspic-update-observation.tsへ検証用入力の抽出・仮旧値構成を分離。--observed-updatesではメール記載値によるAと公開ID/現在値一致を別集計する。ネットワーク・SMTP・DB書込み機能はない。原本とcollector Item配列はGit外の一時領域だけに置く。出力は集計のみ、入力詳細は例外へ表示しない。
+
+~~~bash
+node scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail.ts /private/update-raw.json /private/collected-items.json --observed-updates
+npx vitest run tests/jaspic tests/jaspicShigaMail.test.ts
+~~~
+
+既存新規97通も拡張後の検証器で再照合し、本文・CRLF・件名ヘッダー全件一致を維持した（広島69+19、滋賀9）。滋賀新規は前回読み取った原本を再利用し、今回別アカウントへの接続切替はしていない。
+
+## 型チェックとテスト
+
+変更前head a89958c時点で全体tscの出力を確保し、変更後と比較。既存15件（handler.test.ts 6、mail.test.ts 2、mime.test.ts 5、state.test.ts 2）の診断が同一。原因は異なるJSON fixture形状がunionに推論され、他種別の未定義項目がoptional undefinedとなる一方、RecordDataはstring|nullのみを許すこと。テストのfixture入力の静的型に限る不整合で、ランタイムテスト失敗ではない。今回の変更に由来する診断追加なし。全体型チェック成功とは報告しない。
+
+追加・変更した検証コードとテストはstrict TypeScript単独チェック成功。関連8ファイル208テスト成功（既存125＋追加83）。新規/更新の旧C# golden、既存MIME18パターン、実更新A、マーカー位置異常・欠落・重複拒否を維持。既存型不整合を回避するため本番のRecordData定義を緩めていない。
+
+## 次の工程
+
+広島のA検証は完了。滋賀の実更新メール用Gmailへユーザーが接続を切り替えた後、滋賀本文の更新見出しをページ末尾まで検索し同手順を適用する。B未検証、配送Fromとenvelope、センター最終受信互換、未対応MIME入力、実送信キュー、旧PCとの切替境界は引き続き残る。
+
+実メール送信、SMTP接続、本番Edge deploy、本番DB変更、18時cron有効化、旧Windows停止、PRマージはすべて未実施。PRはDraftを維持。

```

diff --git a/docs/sql/jaspic-isolated-draft.sql b/docs/sql/jaspic-isolated-draft.sql
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..e13f2b79
--- /dev/null
+++ b/docs/sql/jaspic-isolated-draft.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,105 @@
+-- PREPARATION ONLY. Not an automatic migration; do not apply to production without approval.
+begin;
+create table public.jaspic_sources (
+  province text primary key check (province in ('hiroshima','shiga')),
+  revision bigint not null default 0,
+  baseline_complete boolean not null default false,
+  checked_at timestamptz
+);
+insert into public.jaspic_sources(province) values ('hiroshima'),('shiga');
+create table public.jaspic_records (
+  province text not null references public.jaspic_sources(province),
+  record_key text not null,
+  payload jsonb not null check (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object'),
+  primary key (province, record_key)
+);
+create table public.jaspic_deliveries (
+  province text not null references public.jaspic_sources(province),
+  record_key text not null,
+  version text not null check (version ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
+  status text not null default 'DRY_RUN' check (status in ('DRY_RUN','PREPARED','SENDING','SENT','UNKNOWN','CANCELLED')),
+  lease_token uuid,
+  lease_until timestamptz,
+  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
+  primary key (province, record_key, version)
+);
+alter table public.jaspic_sources enable row level security;
+alter table public.jaspic_records enable row level security;
+alter table public.jaspic_deliveries enable row level security;
+revoke all on public.jaspic_sources, public.jaspic_records, public.jaspic_deliveries from public, anon, authenticated;
+grant select, insert, update on public.jaspic_sources, public.jaspic_records, public.jaspic_deliveries to service_role;
+
+create function public.jaspic_snapshot(p_province text) returns jsonb
+language sql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
+  select jsonb_build_object('revision', s.revision, 'baseline', s.baseline_complete,
+    'records', coalesce((select jsonb_object_agg(r.record_key,r.payload) from public.jaspic_records r where r.province=s.province),'{}'::jsonb))
+  from public.jaspic_sources s where s.province=p_province;
+$$;
+
+-- Serializes each province independently. CAS rejects stale plans including simultaneous baseline runs.
+-- Dry-run commits are observations, never a sendable backlog. No mail bodies are stored here.
+create function public.jaspic_commit_dry_run(p_province text, p_revision bigint, p_records jsonb, p_versions jsonb)
+returns bigint language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
+declare s public.jaspic_sources; next_revision bigint;
+begin
+  select * into strict s from public.jaspic_sources where province=p_province for update;
+  if s.revision <> p_revision then raise exception 'Stale JASPIC revision'; end if;
+  if p_records is null or p_versions is null or jsonb_typeof(p_records) <> 'object' or p_records = '{}'::jsonb or jsonb_typeof(p_versions) <> 'array' then
+    raise exception 'Invalid JASPIC snapshot';
+  end if;
+  if not s.baseline_complete and jsonb_array_length(p_versions) <> 0 then raise exception 'Baseline must be silent'; end if;
+  if pg_column_size(p_records) > 16*1024*1024 or
+    (pg_total_relation_size('public.jaspic_records') + pg_total_relation_size('public.jaspic_deliveries')) > 32*1024*1024 then
+    raise exception 'JASPIC capacity guard';
+  end if;
+  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_each(p_records) r where r.key not like p_province || '-%'
+    or jsonb_typeof(r.value) <> 'object' or coalesce(r.value->>'id','') = '') then raise exception 'Invalid source records'; end if;
+  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(p_versions) v where not (p_records ? (v->>'key'))
+    or coalesce(v->>'version','') !~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') then raise exception 'Invalid source versions'; end if;
+  insert into public.jaspic_records(province,record_key,payload)
+    select p_province,key,value from jsonb_each(p_records)
+    on conflict (province,record_key) do update set payload=excluded.payload
+    where public.jaspic_records.payload is distinct from excluded.payload;
+  insert into public.jaspic_deliveries(province,record_key,version,status)
+    select p_province,v->>'key',v->>'version','DRY_RUN' from jsonb_array_elements(p_versions) v
+    on conflict do nothing;
+  update public.jaspic_sources set revision=revision+1, baseline_complete=true, checked_at=now()
+    where province=p_province returning revision into next_revision;
+  return next_revision;
+end;
+$$;
+revoke all on function public.jaspic_snapshot(text), public.jaspic_commit_dry_run(text,bigint,jsonb,jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;
+grant execute on function public.jaspic_snapshot(text), public.jaspic_commit_dry_run(text,bigint,jsonb,jsonb) to service_role;
+
+-- Prepared for later transport only. DRY_RUN rows are deliberately ineligible.
+-- No function in this draft creates PREPARED deliveries, and no sender is included.
+create function public.jaspic_claim(p_province text, p_key text, p_version text, p_token uuid)
+returns boolean language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
+begin
+  if p_token is null then raise exception 'Missing lease token'; end if;
+  update public.jaspic_deliveries set status='SENDING',lease_token=p_token,lease_until=now()+interval '5 minutes'
+    where province=p_province and record_key=p_key and version=p_version and status='PREPARED';
+  return found;
+end;
+$$;
+create function public.jaspic_finish(p_province text, p_key text, p_version text, p_token uuid, p_accepted boolean)
+returns boolean language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
+begin
+  if p_accepted is null then raise exception 'Missing transport outcome'; end if;
+  update public.jaspic_deliveries set status=case when p_accepted then 'SENT' else 'UNKNOWN' end,lease_until=null
+    where province=p_province and record_key=p_key and version=p_version and status='SENDING' and lease_token=p_token and lease_until > now();
+  return found;
+end;
+$$;
+create function public.jaspic_expire_uncertain(p_province text)
+returns bigint language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
+declare n bigint;
+begin
+  update public.jaspic_deliveries set status='UNKNOWN',lease_until=null
+    where province=p_province and status='SENDING' and lease_until <= now();
+  get diagnostics n = row_count; return n;
+end;
+$$;
+revoke all on function public.jaspic_claim(text,text,text,uuid),public.jaspic_finish(text,text,text,uuid,boolean),public.jaspic_expire_uncertain(text) from public,anon,authenticated;
+grant execute on function public.jaspic_claim(text,text,text,uuid),public.jaspic_finish(text,text,text,uuid,boolean),public.jaspic_expire_uncertain(text) to service_role;
+commit;

```

+-- REVIEW TEMPLATE ONLY. This file cannot activate a job as supplied.
+do $$ begin raise exception 'Not authorized: review dry-run deployment and DB changes first'; end $$;
+-- After explicit authorization, use two independent cron.schedule calls below.
+-- Confirm pg_cron's configured timezone is UTC. 09:00 UTC = 18:00 JST.
+-- Store project URL and per-province job tokens in Vault; never place token literals in job text.
+/*
+select cron.schedule('jaspic-hiroshima-dry-run','0 9 * * *',$job$
+ select net.http_post(
+  url := (select decrypted_secret from vault.decrypted_secrets where name='jaspic_project_url') || '/functions/v1/jaspic-hiroshima',
+  headers := jsonb_build_object('Content-Type','application/json',
+    'x-jaspic-token',(select decrypted_secret from vault.decrypted_secrets where name='jaspic_hiroshima_job_token')),
+  body := '{"mode":"dry-run","persist":true}'::jsonb,
+  timeout_milliseconds := 60000);
+$job$);
+select cron.schedule('jaspic-shiga-dry-run','0 9 * * *',$job$
+ select net.http_post(
+  url := (select decrypted_secret from vault.decrypted_secrets where name='jaspic_project_url') || '/functions/v1/jaspic-shiga',
+  headers := jsonb_build_object('Content-Type','application/json',
+    'x-jaspic-token',(select decrypted_secret from vault.decrypted_secrets where name='jaspic_shiga_job_token')),
+  body := '{"mode":"dry-run","persist":true}'::jsonb,
+  timeout_milliseconds := 60000);
+$job$);

### lib/jaspic/shigaMail.ts

```text
diff --git a/lib/jaspic/shigaMail.ts b/lib/jaspic/shigaMail.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..46aab5dc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/jaspic/shigaMail.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,11 @@
+/** Existing public entry point; implementation is shared with the isolated Edge worker. */
+import { generateMail, type RecordData } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail";
+export type ShigaRecord = Record<string, string | null | undefined>;
+export const SHIGA_SUBJECT = " [JASPIC-NES] 滋賀県警専用ツール";
+export const SHIGA_FROM = "hppmap@kishibojim.online";
+export const SHIGA_TO = "getter01@jaspic-nes.net";
+export const SHIGA_CC = "kishibojim@gmail.com";
+const normalize = (data: ShigaRecord): RecordData => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(data).map(([key,value]) => [key,value ?? null]));
+export function renderShigaText(current: ShigaRecord, previous: ShigaRecord | null, cityName: string): string {
+  return generateMail("shiga-person", normalize(current), previous === null ? null : normalize(previous), {cityName,stationName:""}).body;
+}

```

### scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs

```text
diff --git a/scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs b/scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..dc28388f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,26 @@
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+// Deliberately strict: only explicitly volatile transport headers may be excluded.
+// Everything else, including folding, charset, CTE and encoded body bytes, must match.
+const volatile = new Set(['date','message-id','received','return-path','dkim-signature','authentication-results','received-spf','x-google-smtp-source','x-received','x-gm-message-state']);
+export function comparable(buffer, {stage = 'prepared'} = {}) {
+  if (!['prepared','unprepared'].includes(stage)) throw new Error('Invalid MIME comparison stage');
+  const raw=buffer.toString('latin1');
+  if (raw.replaceAll('\r\n','').match(/[\r\n]/)) throw new Error('Non-CRLF message');
+  const split=raw.indexOf('\r\n\r\n');
+  if(split<0) throw new Error('Missing MIME header separator');
+  const headers=raw.slice(0,split).split(/\r\n(?![ \t])/);
+  const names=headers.map(h=>h.slice(0,h.indexOf(':')).toLowerCase());
+  for(const name of ['from','to','cc','subject','mime-version','content-type',...(stage==='prepared'?['content-transfer-encoding']:[])]) {
+    if(names.filter(n=>n===name).length!==1) throw new Error('Missing or duplicate required header');
+  }
+  if(stage==='unprepared' && names.filter(n=>n==='content-transfer-encoding').length>1) throw new Error('Duplicate transfer encoding');
+  return Buffer.from(headers.filter(h=>!volatile.has(h.slice(0,h.indexOf(':')).toLowerCase())).join('\r\n')+'\r\n\r\n'+raw.slice(split+4),'latin1');
+}
+export function compareEml(left,right, options) { return comparable(left,options).equals(comparable(right,options)); }
+if(process.argv[1] && import.meta.url===pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
+  if(process.argv.length!==4 && !(process.argv.length===5 && process.argv[4]==='--unprepared')) throw new Error('Usage: node scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs old.eml new.eml [--unprepared]');
+  const same=compareEml(readFileSync(process.argv[2]),readFileSync(process.argv[3]),{stage:process.argv[4]?'unprepared':'prepared'});
+  console.log(same?'MATCH (volatile transport headers excluded)':'MISMATCH (no message contents printed)');
+  process.exitCode=same?0:1;
+}

```

### scripts/jaspic-dry-run.ts

```text
diff --git a/scripts/jaspic-dry-run.ts b/scripts/jaspic-dry-run.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..036c9704
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/jaspic-dry-run.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,18 @@
+// Read-only public source probe. No DB client, SMTP transport or email API.
+import { collect, type Province } from "../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts";
+import { plan } from "../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state.ts";
+const province = process.argv[2];
+if (province !== "hiroshima" && province !== "shiga") throw new Error("Usage: node scripts/jaspic-dry-run.ts hiroshima|shiga");
+try {
+  const items = await collect(province as Province);
+  const initial = await plan(province, {revision:0,baseline:false,records:{}},items);
+  const repeated = await plan(province, {...initial,revision:1},items);
+  const changed = items.map((item,index)=>index===0?{...item,data:{...item.data,address:(item.data.address??"")+"【合成更新検証】"}}:item);
+  const update = await plan(province,{...initial,revision:1},changed);
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({province,counts:Object.fromEntries([...new Set(items.map(i=>i.kind))].map(kind=>[kind,items.filter(i=>i.kind===kind).length])),
+    baselineCandidates:initial.candidates.length,repeatedCandidates:repeated.candidates.length,syntheticUpdateCandidates:update.candidates.length,
+    comparisonPayloadBytes:Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(initial.records)),sent:0,persisted:false}));
+} catch {
+  console.error(JSON.stringify({province,error:"SOURCE_OR_VALIDATION_FAILED",sent:0,persisted:false}));
+  process.exitCode=1;
+}

```

### scripts/jaspic-generate-eml.ts

```text
diff --git a/scripts/jaspic-generate-eml.ts b/scripts/jaspic-generate-eml.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2fe7f4e4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/jaspic-generate-eml.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+// Offline synthetic fixtures only. No network or environment/secret access.
+import { readFileSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { generateEml, type MimeMode } from "../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime.ts";
+import type { Kind, RecordData } from "../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail.ts";
+if(process.argv.length!==4) throw new Error("Usage: node scripts/jaspic-generate-eml.ts synthetic-fixtures.json output-directory");
+const fixtures=JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.argv[2],"utf8")) as {kind:Kind;current:RecordData;previous:RecordData|null;cityName:string;stationName:string}[];
+mkdirSync(process.argv[3],{recursive:true});
+for(const f of fixtures) for(const mode of ["unprepared","seven-bit","eight-bit"] as MimeMode[]) {
+  if(!["hiroshima-crime","hiroshima-person","shiga-person"].includes(f.kind)) throw new Error("Invalid synthetic kind");
+  const name=f.kind+(f.previous===null?"-new":"-update")+"-"+mode+".eml";
+  writeFileSync(join(process.argv[3],name),generateEml(f.kind,f.current,f.previous,f,mode,{date:"Wed, 04 Nov 2026 18:00:00 +0900",messageId:"synthetic-jaspic@localhost.invalid"}));
+}
+console.log(`Generated ${fixtures.length*3} synthetic EML files; sent=0`);

```

### scripts/jaspic-source-probe.mjs

```text
diff --git a/scripts/jaspic-source-probe.mjs b/scripts/jaspic-source-probe.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..155850ca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/jaspic-source-probe.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+// Read-only public endpoint diagnostics for JASPIC migration.
+// No DB, SMTP, credentials, proxy, browser spoofing, or payload logging.
+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+
+export const MAX_BYTES = 12_000_000;
+export const SOURCES = Object.freeze({
+  cities: 'https://map.police.shiga.dsvc.jp/data/geo/cities.json',
+  persons: 'https://map.police.shiga.dsvc.jp/data/map/suspicious_person_full.json',
+});
+const REQUIRED_PERSON_KEYS = ['id', 'city_code', 'address', 'address_code', 'latitude', 'longitude', 'police_station', 'case_type', 'occurrence_dt', 'accident_situation', 'criminal_car', 'criminal_feature', 'resolution'];
+
+export async function probeSource(name, variant, request = fetch) {
+  if (!Object.hasOwn(SOURCES, name)) throw new Error('Unknown source');
+  if (!['bare-json', 'bare-any', 'cache-bust-json'].includes(variant)) throw new Error('Unknown variant');
+  const url = SOURCES[name] + (variant === 'cache-bust-json' ? '?' + Date.now() : '');
+  const headers = { Accept: variant === 'bare-any' ? '*/*' : 'application/json' };
+  const result = { source: name, variant, outcome: 'failed' };
+  try {
+    const response = await request(url, { method: 'GET', headers, redirect: 'error', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20_000) });
+    result.status = response.status;
+    result.contentType = response.headers.get('content-type')?.slice(0, 120) ?? null;
+    result.contentEncoding = response.headers.get('content-encoding')?.slice(0, 50) ?? null;
+    result.cacheControl = response.headers.get('cache-control')?.slice(0, 120) ?? null;
+    if (!response.ok) { result.outcome = 'http-error'; await response.body?.cancel().catch(() => {}); return result; }
+    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
+    if (!reader) { result.outcome = 'missing-body'; return result; }
+    let size = 0; const chunks = [];
+    try {
+      while (true) {
+        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
+        if (done) break;
+        size += value.byteLength;
+        if (size > MAX_BYTES) { result.outcome = 'too-large'; result.bytesOverLimit = true; return result; }
+        chunks.push(value);
+      }
+    } finally { await reader.cancel().catch(() => {}); }
+    result.bytes = size;
+    const bytes = new Uint8Array(size); let offset = 0;
+    for (const chunk of chunks) { bytes.set(chunk, offset); offset += chunk.byteLength; }
+    let decoded;
+    try { decoded = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes).replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''); }
+    catch { result.outcome = 'invalid-utf8'; return result; }
+    result.replacementChars = (decoded.match(/\uFFFD/g) ?? []).length;
+    let json;
+    try { json = JSON.parse(decoded); }
+    catch { result.outcome = 'invalid-json'; return result; }
+    if (name === 'persons') {
+      if (!Array.isArray(json)) { result.outcome = 'wrong-schema'; return result; }
+      result.records = json.length;
+      result.firstRecordMissingKeys = json.length && json[0] && typeof json[0] === 'object'
+        ? REQUIRED_PERSON_KEYS.filter(k => !Object.hasOwn(json[0], k))
+        : REQUIRED_PERSON_KEYS;
+    } else {
+      if (!json || !Array.isArray(json.features)) { result.outcome = 'wrong-schema'; return result; }
+      result.records = json.features.length;
+    }
+    result.outcome = 'ok';
+    return result;
+  } catch (e) {
+    // Never expose proxy hostnames, URLs, response bodies, stack traces or credentials.
+    result.errorType = e?.name === 'TimeoutError' ? 'timeout' : e?.name === 'AbortError' ? 'aborted' : 'network-error';
+    return result;
+  }
+}
+
+if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
+  for (const name of Object.keys(SOURCES)) {
+    for (const variant of ['bare-json', 'bare-any', 'cache-bust-json']) {
+      console.log(JSON.stringify(await probeSource(name, variant)));
+    }
+  }
+}

```

### scripts/jaspic-source-probe.nodecheck.mjs

```text
diff --git a/scripts/jaspic-source-probe.nodecheck.mjs b/scripts/jaspic-source-probe.nodecheck.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..7a8ba142
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/jaspic-source-probe.nodecheck.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { probeSource, SOURCES } from './jaspic-source-probe.mjs';
+const respond = (body, status = 200, headers = {}) => async () => new Response(body, { status, headers });
+test('HTTP errors do not expose response body', async () => {
+  const r = await probeSource('persons', 'bare-json', respond('private incident body', 404));
+  assert.equal(r.outcome, 'http-error'); assert.equal(r.status, 404);
+  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('private'));
+});
+test('person metadata omits incident content', async () => {
+  const r = await probeSource('persons', 'bare-any', respond(JSON.stringify([{id:'private-id',address:'sensitive address'}])));
+  assert.equal(r.outcome, 'ok'); assert.equal(r.records, 1);
+  assert.ok(r.firstRecordMissingKeys.includes('city_code'));
+  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('private-id'));
+  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(r).includes('sensitive address'));
+});
+test('cities features count', async () => {
+  const r = await probeSource('cities', 'cache-bust-json', respond('{"features":[{},{}]}'));
+  assert.equal(r.outcome, 'ok'); assert.equal(r.records, 2);
+});
+test('invalid JSON and network error are classified', async () => {
+  assert.equal((await probeSource('persons','bare-json',respond('<html>oops</html>'))).outcome, 'invalid-json');
+  assert.equal((await probeSource('persons','bare-json',async () => {throw new Error('private proxy host');})).errorType, 'network-error');
+});
+test('Accept and query variants preserve exact source path', async () => {
+  const calls=[];
+  const fake=async (url, options) => {calls.push({url,accept:options.headers.Accept}); return new Response('[]');};
+  await probeSource('persons','bare-json',fake);
+  await probeSource('persons','bare-any',fake);
+  await probeSource('persons','cache-bust-json',fake);
+  assert.equal(calls[0].url,SOURCES.persons);
+  assert.equal(calls[1].accept,'*/*');
+  assert.ok(calls[2].url.startsWith(SOURCES.persons+'?'));
+});
+
+test('invalid UTF-8 and already-corrupted Unicode are distinct, without logging content', async () => {
+  const invalid = await probeSource('persons', 'bare-json', respond(new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xfe])));
+  assert.equal(invalid.outcome, 'invalid-utf8');
+  const preCorrupted = await probeSource('persons', 'bare-json', respond(JSON.stringify([{id:'private-id',address:String.fromCharCode(0xfffd)}])));
+  assert.equal(preCorrupted.outcome, 'ok');
+  assert.equal(preCorrupted.replacementChars, 1);
+  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(preCorrupted).includes('private-id'));
+});

```

### scripts/jaspic-update-observation.ts

```text
diff --git a/scripts/jaspic-update-observation.ts b/scripts/jaspic-update-observation.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..9643eb3a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/jaspic-update-observation.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+// Test-only reconstruction of rendering inputs; never a restoration of the old DB.
+import { fields, type RecordData, type Kind } from '../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail.ts';
+import type { Item } from '../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts';
+
+export function observedFields(text: string) {
+  const entries = [...text.matchAll(/^"([a-z_]+)":"(.*)"(.*)\r$/gm)];
+  const result = new Map<string, { value: string; annotation: string; marked: boolean }>();
+  for (const m of entries) {
+    if (result.has(m[1])) throw new Error('Duplicate observed field');
+    result.set(m[1], { value: m[2], annotation: m[3].replace(/●更新$/, ''), marked: m[3].endsWith('●更新') });
+  }
+  if ([...result.values()].filter(f => f.marked).length !== (text.match(/●更新/g) ?? []).length) throw new Error('Unrecognized update marker position');
+  return result;
+}
+export function markedPrevious(text: string, kind: Kind, current: RecordData): RecordData {
+  const observed = observedFields(text);
+  if (observed.size !== fields[kind].length || fields[kind].some(k => !observed.has(k))) throw new Error('Incomplete observed fields');
+  const previous = { ...current };
+  for (const key of fields[kind]) {
+    if (observed.get(key)!.marked) previous[key] = (current[key] ?? '') + '[SYNTHETIC_PREVIOUS_VALUE]';
+  }
+  return previous;
+}
+export function observedItem(text: string): Item {
+  const kind: Kind = text.includes('[JASPIC - NES] 滋賀県警アラート') ? 'shiga-person' : text.includes('広島県警が新しい犯罪情報') ? 'hiroshima-crime' : 'hiroshima-person';
+  const observed = observedFields(text);
+  if (observed.size !== fields[kind].length || fields[kind].some(k => !observed.has(k))) throw new Error('Incomplete observed fields');
+  const data: RecordData = Object.fromEntries([...observed].map(([k,v]) => [k,v.value]));
+  // Hiroshima renders the code as a label. Empty labels cannot recover the raw
+  // code (or null); use a rendering-equivalent value, never call it recovered DB.
+  if (kind === 'hiroshima-person') {
+    const codes: Record<string,string> = { '': '', '検挙': '1', '指導・警告': '2', 'その他': '9' };
+    if (!Object.hasOwn(codes, data.resolution!)) throw new Error('Unknown resolution label');
+    data.resolution = codes[data.resolution!];
+  }
+  const annotation = (key: string) => {
+    const value = observed.get(key)!.annotation;
+    const start = value.indexOf('→');
+    if (start < 0) throw new Error('Missing lookup annotation');
+    return value.slice(start + 1);
+  };
+  return { kind, data, context: { cityName: annotation('city_code'), stationName: kind === 'shiga-person' ? '' : annotation('police_station') } };
+}

```

### scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail.ts

```text
diff --git a/scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail.ts b/scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..835078ad
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,93 @@
+// Offline verifier. Inputs remain outside Git; output contains aggregate verdicts only.
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+import { generateMail, type Kind } from '../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail.ts';
+import { markedPrevious, observedItem } from './jaspic-update-observation.ts';
+import { generateEml } from '../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime.ts';
+import type { Item } from '../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts';
+
+export function verifyRealMail(raw: Buffer, items: Item[]) {
+  const split = raw.indexOf('\r\n\r\n');
+  if (split < 0) throw new Error('Missing MIME separator');
+  const headers = raw.subarray(0, split).toString('utf8').split(/\r\n(?![ \t])/);
+  const header = (name: string) => {
+    const values = headers.filter(h => h.slice(0, h.indexOf(':')).toLowerCase() === name);
+    if (values.length !== 1) throw new Error('Missing or duplicate verification header');
+    return values[0].slice(values[0].indexOf(':') + 1).replace(/\r\n[ \t]+/g, ' ').trim();
+  };
+  if (!/^text\/plain;\s*charset="?utf-8"?$/i.test(header('content-type')) || header('content-transfer-encoding').toLowerCase() !== '8bit') {
+    throw new Error('Unsupported MIME profile');
+  }
+  const body = raw.subarray(split + 4);
+  const text = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(body);
+  const kind: Kind = text.includes('[JASPIC - NES] 滋賀県警アラート') ? 'shiga-person' : text.includes('広島県警が新しい犯罪情報') ? 'hiroshima-crime' : 'hiroshima-person';
+  const ids = [...text.matchAll(/^"id":"([^"\r\n]+)"/gm)];
+  if (ids.length !== 1) throw new Error('Missing or ambiguous management ID');
+  const matches = items.filter(i => i.kind === kind && i.data.id === ids[0][1]);
+  if (matches.length !== 1) throw new Error('Missing or ambiguous source record');
+  const item = matches[0];
+  const update = text.includes('アラート（更新）●\r\n');
+  if (!update && !text.includes('アラート（新規）\r\n')) throw new Error('Unknown notification heading');
+  const previous = update ? markedPrevious(text, kind, item.data) : null;
+  const generated = generateMail(kind, item.data, previous, item.context);
+  // Synthetic previous values reproduce observed marker combinations only.
+  // They do not verify real previous values or update detection.
+  const projected = text;
+  const subject = header('subject').replace(/(\?=)\s+(=\?)/g, '$1$2').replace(/=\?utf-8\?b\?([A-Za-z0-9+/=]+)\?=/gi, (_, b64: string) => new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(Buffer.from(b64, 'base64')));
+  const generatedHeaders = Buffer.from(generateEml(kind, item.data, previous, item.context, 'eight-bit', { date: 'Wed, 04 Nov 2026 00:00:00 +0000', messageId: 'verification.invalid' })).toString('utf8').split('\r\n\r\n')[0].split(/\r\n(?![ \t])/);
+  const subjectHeaderBytesMatch = headers.find(h => /^subject:/i.test(h)) === generatedHeaders.find(h => /^subject:/i.test(h));
+  return {
+    kind, update, subjectHeaderBytesMatch,
+    bodyBytesMatch: body.equals(Buffer.from(generated.body, 'utf8')),
+    currentValuesAndStructureMatch: Buffer.from(projected, 'utf8').equals(Buffer.from(generated.body, 'utf8')),
+    crlfOnly: !/[\r\n]/.test(text.replaceAll('\r\n', '')),
+    subjectLogicalMatch: subject === generated.subject,
+    // MimeKit removes the logical leading SP at serialization; report separately.
+    subjectSerializedValueMatch: subject === generated.subject.slice(1),
+    observedUpdateMarkers: (text.match(/●更新(?=\r\n)/g) ?? []).length,
+    previousValuesVerified: false,
+  };
+}
+
+if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
+  try {
+    const observedMode = process.argv[4] === '--observed-updates';
+    if (process.argv.length !== 4 && !(process.argv.length === 5 && observedMode)) throw new Error('Invalid arguments');
+    const mails = JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.argv[2], 'utf8')) as { raw_mime_base64url: string }[];
+    const items = JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.argv[3], 'utf8')) as Item[];
+    const summary: Record<string, Record<string, number>> = {};
+    let failed = false;
+    for (const mail of mails) {
+      if (!mail.raw_mime_base64url) throw new Error('Missing raw MIME');
+      const raw = Buffer.from(mail.raw_mime_base64url, 'base64url');
+      const observed = observedMode ? observedItem(raw.subarray(raw.indexOf('\r\n\r\n') + 4).toString('utf8')) : null;
+      const source = observed ? items.filter(i => i.kind === observed.kind && i.data.id === observed.data.id) : [];
+      if (source.length > 1) throw new Error('Ambiguous source record');
+      const r = verifyRealMail(raw, observed ? [observed] : items);
+      if (observedMode && !r.update) throw new Error('Expected update notification');
+      const group = summary[r.kind] ??= { total: 0, newBodyByteMatches: 0, updates: 0, currentProjectionMatches: 0, crlfMatches: 0, serializedSubjectMatches: 0, logicalSubjectMatches: 0, subjectHeaderByteMatches: 0 };
+      group.total++;
+      if (observedMode) {
+        if (source.length) {
+          group.sourceIdMatches = (group.sourceIdMatches ?? 0) + 1;
+          if (verifyRealMail(raw, source).bodyBytesMatch) group.sourceBodyMatches = (group.sourceBodyMatches ?? 0) + 1;
+        } else group.sourceIdMissing = (group.sourceIdMissing ?? 0) + 1;
+      }
+      if (r.subjectHeaderBytesMatch) group.subjectHeaderByteMatches++;
+      if (r.bodyBytesMatch && !r.update) group.newBodyByteMatches++;
+      if (r.bodyBytesMatch && r.update) group.updateBodyByteMatches = (group.updateBodyByteMatches ?? 0) + 1;
+      if (r.update) group.updates++;
+      if (r.currentValuesAndStructureMatch) group.currentProjectionMatches++;
+      if (r.crlfOnly) group.crlfMatches++;
+      if (r.subjectSerializedValueMatch) group.serializedSubjectMatches++;
+      if (r.subjectLogicalMatch) group.logicalSubjectMatches++;
+      failed ||= !r.currentValuesAndStructureMatch || !r.crlfOnly || !r.subjectSerializedValueMatch || !r.subjectHeaderBytesMatch;
+    }
+    if (!mails.length) throw new Error('Empty verification set');
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({ summary, previousValuesVerified: false, sent: 0, persisted: false }));
+    process.exitCode = failed ? 1 : 0;
+  } catch {
+    console.error('VERIFICATION_FAILED (private input details suppressed)');
+    process.exitCode = 1;
+  }
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..041ffa56
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@
+import { fields, generateMail, type Kind, type MailContext, type RecordData } from "./mail.ts";
+
+export type Province = "hiroshima" | "shiga";
+export type Item = { kind: Kind; data: RecordData; context: MailContext };
+const base = (p: Province) => `https://map.police.${p}.dsvc.jp`;
+export const endpoints = {
+  hiroshima: ["/data/geo/cities_master.json", "/data/json/police.json", "/data/map/suspicious_person3.json", "/data/map/criminals.json"],
+  shiga: ["/data/geo/cities.json", "/data/map/suspicious_person_full.json"],
+};
+export async function readJson(url: string, request: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<unknown> {
+  const response = await request(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20_000), redirect: "error", headers: { Accept: "application/json" } });
+  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Source HTTP ${response.status}`);
+  // Bound memory even when Content-Length is missing or dishonest.
+  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
+  if (!reader) throw new Error("Missing source body");
+  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = []; let size = 0;
+  try {
+    while (true) {
+      const part = await reader.read(); if (part.done) break;
+      size += part.value.length;
+      if (size > 12_000_000) throw new Error("Source too large");
+      chunks.push(part.value);
+    }
+  } finally { await reader.cancel(); }
+  const bytes = new Uint8Array(size); let offset = 0;
+  for (const chunk of chunks) { bytes.set(chunk, offset); offset += chunk.length; }
+  return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes).replace(/^\uFEFF/, ""));
+}
+function rows(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown>[] {
+  if (!Array.isArray(value) || !value.length || value.some(v => !v || typeof v !== "object" || Array.isArray(v))) throw new Error("Incomplete source collection");
+  return value;
+}
+function text(value: unknown): string {
+  if (typeof value !== "string" && typeof value !== "number") throw new Error("Invalid lookup value");
+  return String(value);
+}
+function records(kind: Kind, value: unknown): RecordData[] {
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  return rows(value).map(row => {
+    const data: RecordData = {};
+    for (const key of fields[kind]) {
+      if (!Object.hasOwn(row, key) || (row[key] !== null && typeof row[key] !== "string")) throw new Error("Source schema changed");
+      data[key] = row[key] === null ? null : String(row[key]);
+    }
+    if (!data.id || seen.has(data.id)) throw new Error("Missing or duplicate source ID");
+    seen.add(data.id); return data;
+  });
+}
+export async function collect(province: Province, request: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<Item[]> {
+  const values = await Promise.all(endpoints[province].map(path => readJson(base(province) + path + "?" + Date.now(), request)));
+  const items: Item[] = [];
+  if (province === "hiroshima") {
+    const cities = rows(values[0]); const stations = rows(values[1]);
+    for (const [kind, value] of [["hiroshima-person", values[2]], ["hiroshima-crime", values[3]]] as const) {
+      for (const data of records(kind, value)) {
+        const city = cities.find(c => text(c.city_code) === data.city_code);
+        const station = stations.find(s => text(s.station_id) === data.police_station);
+        if (!city || !station) throw new Error("Missing source lookup");
+        items.push({ kind, data, context: { cityName: text(city.city_name), stationName: text(station.station_name) } });
+      }
+    }
+  } else {
+    const geo = values[0] as { features?: unknown };
+    const cities = rows(geo?.features);
+    for (const data of records("shiga-person", values[1])) {
+      const city = cities.find(c => text((c.properties as Record<string, unknown>)?.city_code) === data.city_code);
+      if (!city) throw new Error("Missing source lookup");
+      items.push({ kind: "shiga-person", data, context: { cityName: text((city.properties as Record<string, unknown>).full_name), stationName: "" } });
+    }
+  }
+  // Validate every body before a caller can commit a partial baseline.
+  for (const item of items) generateMail(item.kind, item.data, null, item.context);
+  return items;
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.ts

diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..ee51a06e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+/**
+ * Gmail API request construction ONLY; no fetch, OAuth execution, SMTP or secrets storage.
+ * The result contains a bearer token: never serialize it to logs, GitHub or diagnostics.
+ * Not connected to any Edge Function.
+ */
+export type GmailSendRequestOffline = {
+  url: "https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/send";
+  method: "POST";
+  headers: { Authorization: string; "Content-Type": "application/json" };
+  body: string;
+};
+export function prepareGmailSendRequestOffline(
+  raw: string, accessToken: string,
+): GmailSendRequestOffline {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(raw) || raw.length > 15_000_000) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid encoded MIME payload");
+  }
+  if (!accessToken || accessToken.length > 8192 || /[\r\n\0\s]/.test(accessToken)) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid OAuth access token");
+  }
+  return {
+    url: "https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/send",
+    method: "POST",
+    headers: {
+      Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
+      "Content-Type": "application/json",
+    },
+    body: JSON.stringify({ raw }),
+  };
+}

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..e629043d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
+/**
+ * Offline-only Gmail OAuth2 configuration and response validation.
+ * This module has NO fetch, SMTP, token persistence, or send capability.
+ * Never log credentials or return them as part of error strings.
+ */
+export const GMAIL_SEND_SCOPE = "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send" as const;
+export type GmailOAuthSecrets = {
+  clientId: string;
+  clientSecret: string;
+  refreshToken: string;
+};
+export type GmailTokenRequest = {
+  url: "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token";
+  method: "POST";
+  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" };
+  body: string;
+};
+function required(value: string): string {
+  if (!value || value.trim() !== value || /[\r\n\0]/.test(value)) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth configuration");
+  return value;
+}
+/** Builds an inert request descriptor. The caller MUST NOT execute it in production without separate approval. */
+export function prepareRefreshRequestOffline(secrets: GmailOAuthSecrets): GmailTokenRequest {
+  const params = new URLSearchParams({
+    client_id: required(secrets.clientId),
+    client_secret: required(secrets.clientSecret),
+    refresh_token: required(secrets.refreshToken),
+    grant_type: "refresh_token",
+  });
+  return {
+    url: "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token",
+    method: "POST",
+    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
+    body: params.toString(),
+  };
+}
+export type GmailAccessToken = { accessToken: string; expiresInSeconds: number; tokenType: "Bearer" };
+/** No network and no secret-bearing error messages. */
+export function validateRefreshResponseOffline(input: unknown): GmailAccessToken {
+  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth response");
+  const value = input as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (typeof value.access_token !== "string" || !value.access_token ||
+      value.token_type !== "Bearer" ||
+      typeof value.expires_in !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value.expires_in) ||
+      value.expires_in < 1 || value.expires_in > 86400) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid OAuth response");
+  }
+  return { accessToken: required(value.access_token), expiresInSeconds: value.expires_in, tokenType: "Bearer" };
+}
+/** Minimal state-only guard; requires separate persistent transactional implementation before delivery. */
+export type DeliveryState = "NEW" | "LEASED" | "ACCEPTED" | "UNKNOWN" | "FAILED";
+export function nextDeliveryState(current: DeliveryState, action: "lease" | "accepted" | "unknown" | "failed"): DeliveryState {
+  const allowed: Record<DeliveryState, Partial<Record<typeof action, DeliveryState>>> = {
+    NEW: { lease: "LEASED" },
+    LEASED: { accepted: "ACCEPTED", unknown: "UNKNOWN", failed: "FAILED" },
+    ACCEPTED: {}, UNKNOWN: {}, FAILED: {},
+  };
+  const next = allowed[current]?.[action];
+  if (!next) throw new Error("Invalid delivery transition; automatic retry prohibited");
+  return next;
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2c04288a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,34 @@
+/** Offline Gmail API payload preparation only. NO HTTP, OAuth, SMTP or send operation. */
+export const JASPIC_GMAIL_FROM = "miyazaki1016@gmail.com" as const;
+const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
+function base64Url(input: Uint8Array): string {
+  let result = "";
+  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i += 3) {
+    const n = (input[i] << 16) | ((input[i + 1] ?? 0) << 8) | (input[i + 2] ?? 0);
+    result += alphabet[n >>> 18] + alphabet[(n >>> 12) & 63];
+    result += i + 1 < input.length ? alphabet[(n >>> 6) & 63] : "=";
+    result += i + 2 < input.length ? alphabet[n & 63] : "=";
+  }
+  return result.replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
+}
+/** Prepares a payload shape for Gmail API; cannot submit it to any endpoint. */
+export function prepareGmailRawOffline(legacyEml: Uint8Array): { raw: string; from: typeof JASPIC_GMAIL_FROM } {
+  const decoded = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(legacyEml);
+  const boundary = decoded.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
+  if (boundary < 0 || /(?<!\r)\n|\r(?!\n)/.test(decoded)) throw new Error("Invalid MIME line endings");
+  const header = decoded.slice(0, boundary);
+  const body = decoded.slice(boundary + 4);
+  const lines = header.split("\r\n");
+  if (lines.some(line => /^[ \t]/.test(line))) throw new Error("Folded headers require review");
+  const fromLines = lines.filter(line => /^From:/i.test(line));
+  if (fromLines.length !== 1 || fromLines[0] !== "From: hppmap@kishibojim.online") throw new Error("Unexpected legacy sender");
+  for (const name of ["To", "Cc", "Subject", "Content-Type", "MIME-Version"]) {
+    if (lines.filter(line => line.startsWith(name + ":")).length !== 1) throw new Error("Invalid required header");
+  }
+  if (lines.some(line => /^(Bcc|Return-Path|Sender|Reply-To):/i.test(line))) throw new Error("Unreviewed routing header");
+  const changedHeader = lines.map(line => line === fromLines[0] ? "From: " + JASPIC_GMAIL_FROM : line).join("\r\n");
+  const eml = changedHeader + "\r\n\r\n" + body;
+  if (eml.slice(eml.indexOf("\r\n\r\n") + 4) !== body) throw new Error("Body changed");
+  return { raw: base64Url(encoder.encode(eml)), from: JASPIC_GMAIL_FROM };
+}

```
