```text
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+/**
+ * Offline-only Google OpenID Connect sender-identity verification.
+ * Uses the minimal `openid email` scopes alongside gmail.send.
+ * Never obtains mailbox contents, sends mail, or logs OAuth tokens.
+ */
+export const GOOGLE_USERINFO_ENDPOINT = "https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo" as const;
+
+export function validateGoogleSenderIdentityOffline(
+  response: unknown,
+  expectedEmail: string,
+): { googleSubject: string; verifiedEmail: string } {
+  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(expectedEmail) ||
+      expectedEmail.length > 254 ||
+      !response || typeof response !== "object" || Array.isArray(response)) {
+    throw new Error("Google account identity unavailable");
+  }
+  const data = response as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (typeof data.sub !== "string" || !/^[0-9]{1,255}$/.test(data.sub) ||
+      data.email_verified !== true ||
+      typeof data.email !== "string" || data.email.length > 254 ||
+      data.email.toLowerCase() !== expectedEmail.toLowerCase()) {
+    throw new Error("Google account identity unavailable");
+  }
+  return { googleSubject: data.sub, verifiedEmail: data.email };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..6dfca53f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+/**
+ * Offline-only server-authenticated JASPIC OAuth administrator authorization.
+ * The caller MUST obtain identity by independently verifying a server-side
+ * session/JWT. Never pass identity values from query/body/cookies as trusted.
+ * No network, database connection, Gmail token handling or mail sending.
+ */
+export type TrustedServerIdentity = Readonly<{
+  /** Stable Supabase Auth subject, extracted after server-side JWT verification. */
+  subject: string;
+  /** Verified email from the authoritative identity provider, not user_metadata. */
+  verifiedEmail: string;
+  /** Issuer associated with a verified JWT/session. */
+  issuer: string;
+  /** Indicates upstream server-side signature, audience and expiry verification. */
+  sessionVerified: true;
+}>;
+
+export type AdminAuthorizationConfig = Readonly<{
+  expectedSubject: string;
+  expectedEmail: string;
+  expectedIssuer: string;
+}>;
+
+export type ApprovedJaspicAdmin = Readonly<{
+  ownerId: string;
+}>;
+
+function assertIdentifier(value: string): void {
+  if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{8,128}$/.test(value)) throw new Error("JASPIC admin access denied");
+}
+
+/**
+ * Fail-closed local policy on top of independently verified identity.
+ * This function does NOT itself verify a JWT or authenticate a user.
+ */
+export function authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(
+  identity: TrustedServerIdentity | null,
+  config: AdminAuthorizationConfig,
+): ApprovedJaspicAdmin {
+  if (!identity || identity.sessionVerified !== true ||
+      !config.expectedIssuer || !config.expectedEmail ||
+      !identity.issuer || !identity.verifiedEmail) {
+    throw new Error("JASPIC admin access denied");
+  }
+  assertIdentifier(identity.subject);
+  assertIdentifier(config.expectedSubject);
+  if (identity.subject !== config.expectedSubject ||
+      identity.verifiedEmail !== config.expectedEmail ||
+      identity.issuer !== config.expectedIssuer) {
+    throw new Error("JASPIC admin access denied");
+  }
+  return { ownerId: identity.subject };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..92510bce
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+/**
+ * Persistence adapter contract for one-time Gmail OAuth state consumption.
+ * IMPORTANT: no in-memory Map fallback, network I/O, or real DB calls.
+ *
+ * A production adapter must atomically consume a hashed state in a shared
+ * transaction with the expected owner; do not use a SELECT-then-DELETE.
+ */
+export type OAuthOnceOutcome = "consumed" | "invalid_or_expired";
+export type OAuthOnceChallenge = {
+  stateHash: string;
+  ownerId: string;
+  expiresAt: number;
+};
+export type OAuthOnceConsumeRequest = { stateHash: string; ownerId: string; now: number };
+
+/** Database transaction semantics required by a future persistent implementation. */
+export interface OAuthOnceStore {
+  /** Exactly one winner, even across instances and concurrent callbacks. */
+  consumeAtomically(input: OAuthOnceConsumeRequest): Promise<OAuthOnceOutcome>;
+  /** Reject duplicate state hash, never overwrite an existing transaction. */
+  create(input: OAuthOnceChallenge): Promise<void>;
+}
+
+function toHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
+}
+
+/** Hash state before storing it in the database; raw state stays in the sealed cookie. */
+export async function hashGmailOAuthStateOffline(state: string): Promise<string> {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,100}$/.test(state)) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth state");
+  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(state));
+  return toHex(new Uint8Array(hash));
+}
+
+export function validateOAuthOwnerIdOffline(ownerId: string): string {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$/.test(ownerId)) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth owner");
+  return ownerId;
+}
+
+export async function registerOAuthStateOffline(
+  store: OAuthOnceStore,
+  state: string,
+  ownerId: string,
+  expiresAt: number,
+): Promise<void> {
+  validateOAuthOwnerIdOffline(ownerId);
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt) || expiresAt <= 0) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth expiry");
+  await store.create({ stateHash: await hashGmailOAuthStateOffline(state), ownerId, expiresAt });
+}
+
+export async function consumeOAuthStateOffline(
+  store: OAuthOnceStore,
+  state: string,
+  ownerId: string,
+  now = Date.now(),
+): Promise<boolean> {
+  validateOAuthOwnerIdOffline(ownerId);
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now <= 0) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth clock");
+  const response = await store.consumeAtomically({
+    stateHash: await hashGmailOAuthStateOffline(state), ownerId, now,
+  });
+  if (response !== "consumed" && response !== "invalid_or_expired") {
+    throw new Error("Unexpected OAuth state store response");
+  }
+  return response === "consumed";
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..16886619
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+/**
+ * Supabase Auth identity adapter for a future JASPIC admin-only OAuth route.
+ * Not invoked by a deployed HTTP endpoint. No credentials or Google calls.
+ *
+ * IMPORTANT: instantiate the injected auth client on the server with a trusted
+ * Supabase project URL and anon key. The caller MUST pass an access token
+ * extracted from its Authorization header, never an unverified client claim.
+ * Supabase auth.getUser(token) makes a remote Auth request to validate it.
+ */
+import type { TrustedServerIdentity } from "./gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts";
+
+type AuthUser = {
+  id: string;
+  email?: string | null;
+  email_confirmed_at?: string | null;
+};
+type AuthGetUserResponse = {
+  data: { user: AuthUser | null };
+  error: { message: string } | null;
+};
+
+export interface SupabaseAuthVerifier {
+  auth: {
+    getUser(accessToken: string): Promise<AuthGetUserResponse>;
+  };
+}
+
+export type SupabaseIdentityConfig = {
+  /** Must be an exact trusted Supabase Auth issuer, configured server-side. */
+  issuer: string;
+};
+
+/**
+ * Returns a trusted identity only after remote Supabase Auth getUser succeeds.
+ * Does not take email/subject from a request body, query string or JWT decode.
+ */
+export async function verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(
+  supabase: SupabaseAuthVerifier,
+  accessToken: string,
+  config: SupabaseIdentityConfig,
+): Promise<TrustedServerIdentity> {
+  const deny = (): never => { throw new Error("JASPIC login required"); };
+  if (!accessToken || accessToken.length > 8192 || /[\r\n\0]/.test(accessToken) ||
+      !/^https:\/\/[a-z0-9-]+\.supabase\.co\/auth\/v1$/.test(config.issuer)) deny();
+  const result: AuthGetUserResponse = await supabase.auth.getUser(accessToken).catch(() => deny());
+  if (result.error || !result.data?.user) throw new Error("JASPIC login required");
+  const user = result.data.user;
+  if (!/^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$/.test(user.id) ||
+      typeof user.email !== "string" ||
+      !user.email_confirmed_at ||
+      Number.isNaN(Date.parse(user.email_confirmed_at))) throw new Error("JASPIC login required");
+  return {
+    subject: user.id,
+    verifiedEmail: user.email,
+    issuer: config.issuer,
+    sessionVerified: true,
+  };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..09335f1d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+/**
+ * Supabase persistence adapter for one-time JASPIC OAuth state, prepared but
+ * not wired into any deployed route. ONLY instantiate on an authenticated
+ * server with an appropriately restricted service-role client.
+ *
+ * DB RPC performs the actual atomic consume; the JS adapter does not
+ * simulate a transaction or trust the application server's clock.
+ */
+import type {
+  OAuthOnceChallenge, OAuthOnceConsumeRequest, OAuthOnceOutcome, OAuthOnceStore,
+} from "./gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts";
+
+type DbResult<T> = { data: T | null; error: { message: string } | null };
+type InsertTarget = {
+  insert: (input: { state_hash: string; owner_id: string; expires_at: string }) =>
+    PromiseLike<DbResult<unknown>>;
+};
+/** Narrow interface allows unit-testing without real credentials or DB traffic. */
+export interface JaspicOAuthDbClient {
+  from(table: "jaspic_gmail_oauth_states"): InsertTarget;
+  rpc(
+    fn: "jaspic_consume_gmail_oauth_state",
+    params: { p_state_hash: string; p_owner_id: string },
+  ): PromiseLike<DbResult<boolean>>;
+}
+
+function fail(): never {
+  // Avoid leaking DB error details or request identifiers into logs/UI.
+  throw new Error("JASPIC OAuth state storage unavailable");
+}
+
+export function createSupabaseOAuthOnceStore(db: JaspicOAuthDbClient): OAuthOnceStore {
+  return {
+    async create(input: OAuthOnceChallenge): Promise<void> {
+      const isoDate = new Date(input.expiresAt);
+      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(input.expiresAt) ||
+          Number.isNaN(isoDate.getTime()) ||
+          !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(input.stateHash) ||
+          !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$/.test(input.ownerId)) fail();
+      try {
+        const result = await db.from("jaspic_gmail_oauth_states").insert({
+          state_hash: input.stateHash, owner_id: input.ownerId,
+          expires_at: isoDate.toISOString(),
+        });
+        if (result.error) fail();
+      } catch {
+        fail();
+      }
+    },
+    async consumeAtomically(input: OAuthOnceConsumeRequest): Promise<OAuthOnceOutcome> {
+      if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(input.stateHash) ||
+          !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$/.test(input.ownerId) ||
+          !Number.isSafeInteger(input.now)) fail();
+      try {
+        const result = await db.rpc("jaspic_consume_gmail_oauth_state", {
+          p_state_hash: input.stateHash,
+          p_owner_id: input.ownerId,
+        });
+        if (result.error || typeof result.data !== "boolean") fail();
+        return result.data ? "consumed" : "invalid_or_expired";
+      } catch {
+        fail();
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..e2c293ee
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+/**
+ * REVIEW-ONLY token-vault adapter. No live DB calls unless a server-side
+ * authorized caller explicitly injects a client after migration approval.
+ * Never return plaintext refresh tokens to an HTTP response or logs.
+ */
+import type { EncryptedRefreshToken } from "./gmail-oauth-token-vault-offline.ts";
+
+export type VaultRecord = {
+  owner_id: string;
+  key_id: string;
+  encrypted_token: EncryptedRefreshToken;
+  revoked_at: string | null;
+};
+export type VaultDbResult<T> = { data: T | null; error: { message: string } | null };
+export interface JaspicVaultDbClient {
+  from(table: "jaspic_gmail_oauth_vault"): {
+    upsert(record: VaultRecord, options: {onConflict: "owner_id"}): PromiseLike<VaultDbResult<unknown>>;
+    select(columns: "owner_id,key_id,encrypted_token,revoked_at"): {
+      eq(column: "owner_id", value: string): {
+        is(column: "revoked_at", value: null): {
+          maybeSingle(): PromiseLike<VaultDbResult<VaultRecord>>;
+        };
+      };
+    };
+  };
+}
+function validOwner(ownerId: string) {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$/.test(ownerId)) throw new Error("JASPIC token vault unavailable");
+}
+function validEnvelope(value: EncryptedRefreshToken): boolean {
+  return value?.version === 1 && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{4,64}$/.test(value.keyId) &&
+    typeof value.iv === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,64}$/.test(value.iv) &&
+    typeof value.ciphertext === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,8192}$/.test(value.ciphertext);
+}
+
+/** Storage receives encrypted envelope only, never a raw Google token. */
+export async function persistEncryptedRefreshToken(
+  db: JaspicVaultDbClient,
+  ownerId: string,
+  encrypted: EncryptedRefreshToken,
+): Promise<void> {
+  validOwner(ownerId);
+  if (!validEnvelope(encrypted)) throw new Error("JASPIC token vault unavailable");
+  try {
+    const {error} = await db.from("jaspic_gmail_oauth_vault").upsert({
+      owner_id: ownerId,
+      key_id: encrypted.keyId,
+      encrypted_token: encrypted,
+      revoked_at: null,
+    }, {onConflict:"owner_id"});
+    if (error) throw new Error();
+  } catch {
+    throw new Error("JASPIC token vault unavailable");
+  }
+}
+
+/** Returns ciphertext envelope to server-side authorized code only. */
+export async function fetchEncryptedRefreshToken(
+  db: JaspicVaultDbClient,
+  ownerId: string,
+): Promise<EncryptedRefreshToken | null> {
+  validOwner(ownerId);
+  try {
+    const {data,error} = await db.from("jaspic_gmail_oauth_vault")
+      .select("owner_id,key_id,encrypted_token,revoked_at")
+      .eq("owner_id",ownerId)
+      .is("revoked_at",null)
+      .maybeSingle();
+    if (error) throw new Error();
+    if (data === null) return null;
+    if (data.owner_id !== ownerId || data.revoked_at !== null ||
+        !validEnvelope(data.encrypted_token) || data.key_id !== data.encrypted_token.keyId) throw new Error();
+    return data.encrypted_token;
+  } catch {
+    throw new Error("JASPIC token vault unavailable");
+  }
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..4a52cdc5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+/**
+ * Pure, offline Gmail delivery simulator.
+ *
+ * No HTTP, SMTP, OAuth, environment reads, timers or secret access.
+ * This is NOT a production transport. Only canned simulated results are accepted.
+ */
+import { nextDeliveryState, type DeliveryState } from "./gmail-auth-offline.ts";
+
+export type SimulatedGmailResult =
+  | { scenario: "accepted"; syntheticMessageId: string }
+  | { scenario: "rejected" }
+  | { scenario: "timeout" };
+
+export type SimulatedDelivery = {
+  key: string;
+  state: DeliveryState;
+  attempted: boolean;
+  syntheticMessageId?: string;
+};
+
+function validateKey(key: string): void {
+  if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9:_-]{1,180}$/.test(key)) throw new Error("Invalid delivery key");
+}
+
+/**
+ * Executes exactly one simulated delivery attempt. Callers must supply a
+ * persistent deduplicated state in actual delivery design; the simulator
+ * does NOT provide database locking or a real delivery guarantee.
+ */
+export function simulateGmailDelivery(
+  record: Readonly<SimulatedDelivery>, result: SimulatedGmailResult,
+): SimulatedDelivery {
+  validateKey(record.key);
+  if (record.state !== "NEW" || record.attempted) throw new Error("Delivery already attempted or blocked");
+  const leased = nextDeliveryState(record.state, "lease");
+  if (result.scenario === "accepted") {
+    if (!/^synthetic-[a-z0-9-]{1,120}$/.test(result.syntheticMessageId)) {
+      throw new Error("Only synthetic Gmail IDs are accepted");
+    }
+    return {
+      key: record.key,
+      state: nextDeliveryState(leased, "accepted"),
+      attempted: true,
+      syntheticMessageId: result.syntheticMessageId,
+    };
+  }
+  if (result.scenario === "rejected") {
+    return { key: record.key, state: nextDeliveryState(leased, "failed"), attempted: true };
+  }
+  if (result.scenario === "timeout") {
+    return { key: record.key, state: nextDeliveryState(leased, "unknown"), attempted: true };
+  }
+  throw new Error("Unknown simulation scenario");
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..6a8d0f81
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+/**
+ * Composes offline OAuth callback checks, for a future server-only handler.
+ * No HTTP route, Google request, credentials, token storage or mail sending.
+ *
+ * WARNING: supplying a TrustedServerIdentity requires independently verified
+ * server-side auth (signature/aud/iss/expiry). The DB adapter must be a durable
+ * atomic consume, NOT an in-memory store. Do not expose the returned code.
+ */
+import { authorizeJaspicAdminOffline, type AdminAuthorizationConfig, type TrustedServerIdentity } from "./gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts";
+import { consumeOAuthStateOffline, type OAuthOnceStore } from "./gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts";
+import { validateGmailWebCallbackOffline } from "./gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts";
+import { openGmailWebSessionOffline } from "./gmail-web-session-offline.ts";
+
+export type OfflineCallbackInput = Readonly<{
+  callbackUrl: string;
+  encryptedCookie: string;
+  cookieEncryptionKey: string;
+  /** Identified by the trusted HTTP auth adapter, never by query params. */
+  identity: TrustedServerIdentity | null;
+  adminPolicy: AdminAuthorizationConfig;
+  store: OAuthOnceStore;
+  now?: number;
+}>;
+export type VerifiedOfflineCallback = Readonly<{
+  authorizationCode: string;
+  codeVerifier: string;
+  ownerId: string;
+}>;
+
+export async function verifyGmailWebCallbackOffline(
+  input: OfflineCallbackInput,
+): Promise<VerifiedOfflineCallback> {
+  const admin = authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(input.identity, input.adminPolicy);
+  const parsed = new URL(input.callbackUrl);
+  if (parsed.searchParams.getAll("state").length !== 1) {
+    throw new Error("OAuth callback rejected");
+  }
+  const state = parsed.searchParams.get("state") ?? "";
+  const session = await openGmailWebSessionOffline(
+    input.encryptedCookie, state, input.cookieEncryptionKey, input.now,
+  );
+  const callback = validateGmailWebCallbackOffline(input.callbackUrl, session.state);
+  const consumed = await consumeOAuthStateOffline(
+    input.store, session.state, admin.ownerId, input.now,
+  );
+  if (!consumed) throw new Error("OAuth callback rejected");
+  // Server-only values: NEVER serialize these into HTML, JSON or logs.
+  return { authorizationCode: callback.code, codeVerifier: session.codeVerifier, ownerId: admin.ownerId };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-exchange-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-exchange-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-exchange-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..0bb4ffca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-exchange-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+/**
+ * Offline-only Google web OAuth authorization code exchange preparation.
+ * Does NOT call Google, read secrets, store credentials or enable mail sending.
+ * Never log request bodies, OAuth authorization codes or token responses.
+ */
+import { JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK } from "./gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts";
+
+export type WebOAuthExchangeRequest = {
+  url: "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token";
+  method: "POST";
+  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" };
+  body: string;
+};
+
+export type WebOAuthCredentials = {
+  clientId: string;
+  clientSecret: string;
+  code: string;
+  codeVerifier: string;
+};
+
+/** Caller may only execute this in a separately authorized server-side workflow. */
+export function prepareWebOAuthExchangeOffline(credentials: WebOAuthCredentials): WebOAuthExchangeRequest {
+  const { clientId, clientSecret, code, codeVerifier } = credentials;
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/.test(clientId) ||
+      !clientSecret || clientSecret.length > 8192 || /[\r\n\0]/.test(clientSecret) ||
+      !/^[A-Za-z0-9_\/.~-]{1,4096}$/.test(code) ||
+      !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43,128}$/.test(codeVerifier)) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid web OAuth exchange input");
+  }
+  const form = new URLSearchParams({
+    client_id: clientId,
+    client_secret: clientSecret,
+    code,
+    code_verifier: codeVerifier,
+    redirect_uri: JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK,
+    grant_type: "authorization_code",
+  });
+  return {
+    url: "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token",
+    method: "POST",
+    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
+    body: form.toString(),
+  };
+}
+
+export type WebOAuthTokenResponse = {
+  accessToken: string;
+  refreshToken: string;
+  expiresInSeconds: number;
+  tokenType: "Bearer";
+  scope: string;
+};
+
+/**
+ * A first-time consent must yield a refresh token. Do not silently accept
+ * an access-token-only response for unattended daily operation.
+ */
+export function validateWebOAuthTokenOffline(input: unknown): WebOAuthTokenResponse {
+  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid web OAuth token response");
+  }
+  const obj = input as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (typeof obj.access_token !== "string" || !obj.access_token ||
+      typeof obj.refresh_token !== "string" || !obj.refresh_token ||
+      typeof obj.expires_in !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(obj.expires_in) ||
+      obj.expires_in < 1 || obj.expires_in > 86400 || obj.token_type !== "Bearer" ||
+      typeof obj.scope !== "string" ||
+      (!obj.scope.split(" ").includes("https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send") ||
+       !obj.scope.split(" ").includes("openid") ||
+       !obj.scope.split(" ").includes("email"))) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid web OAuth token response");
+  }
+  return {
+    accessToken: obj.access_token,
+    refreshToken: obj.refresh_token,
+    expiresInSeconds: obj.expires_in,
+    tokenType: "Bearer",
+    scope: obj.scope,
+  };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..88395d04
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+/**
+ * iPhone/browser-compatible Gmail OAuth2 Authorization Code + PKCE preparation.
+ * Pure/offline: no fetch, no token exchange, no cookies, no secrets, no sending.
+ *
+ * A production web callback MUST be guarded by authenticated admin access,
+ * a short-lived HttpOnly Secure SameSite=Lax state/verifier cookie or server state,
+ * single-use CSRF validation and server-only secret storage before activation.
+ */
+import { GMAIL_SEND_SCOPE } from "./gmail-auth-offline.ts";
+
+export const JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK = "https://emergencyalert.news/jaspic/oauth/callback" as const;
+
+function base64url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
+  let out = "";
+  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 3) {
+    const n = (bytes[i] << 16) | ((bytes[i + 1] ?? 0) << 8) | (bytes[i + 2] ?? 0);
+    out += alphabet[n >>> 18] + alphabet[(n >>> 12) & 63];
+    out += i + 1 < bytes.length ? alphabet[(n >>> 6) & 63] : "=";
+    out += i + 2 < bytes.length ? alphabet[n & 63] : "=";
+  }
+  return out.replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
+}
+function randomUrlSafe(bytes: number): string {
+  const arr = new Uint8Array(bytes);
+  crypto.getRandomValues(arr);
+  return base64url(arr);
+}
+export type OfflineWebOAuthStart = {
+  authorizationUrl: string;
+  state: string;
+  codeVerifier: string;
+  callback: typeof JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK;
+};
+/**
+ * Creates an authorization URL for a new WEB application OAuth client.
+ * Never put codeVerifier in a URL, browser localStorage, or GitHub.
+ */
+export async function prepareGmailWebConsentOffline(clientId: string): Promise<OfflineWebOAuthStart> {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/.test(clientId)) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid OAuth web client ID");
+  }
+  const codeVerifier = randomUrlSafe(48);
+  const state = randomUrlSafe(32);
+  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(codeVerifier));
+  const challenge = base64url(new Uint8Array(digest));
+  const query = new URLSearchParams({
+    client_id: clientId,
+    redirect_uri: JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK,
+    response_type: "code",
+    scope: "openid email " + GMAIL_SEND_SCOPE,
+    access_type: "offline",
+    prompt: "consent",
+    state,
+    code_challenge: challenge,
+    code_challenge_method: "S256",
+  });
+  return { authorizationUrl: "https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth?" + query, state, codeVerifier, callback: JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK };
+}
+export type OfflineCallback = { code: string };
+/** Validates callback parameters only; does NOT exchange a code. */
+export function validateGmailWebCallbackOffline(url: string, expectedState: string): OfflineCallback {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,100}$/.test(expectedState)) throw new Error("Invalid stored OAuth state");
+  let parsed: URL;
+  try { parsed = new URL(url); } catch { throw new Error("Invalid OAuth callback URL"); }
+  if (parsed.origin !== "https://emergencyalert.news" || parsed.pathname !== "/jaspic/oauth/callback" ||
+      parsed.hash || parsed.searchParams.getAll("state").length !== 1 ||
+      parsed.searchParams.getAll("code").length !== 1 || parsed.searchParams.has("error") ||
+      parsed.searchParams.get("state") !== expectedState) {
+    throw new Error("OAuth callback refused");
+  }
+  const code = parsed.searchParams.get("code") ?? "";
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_\/.~-]{1,4096}$/.test(code)) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth authorization code");
+  return { code };
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..d030c683
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+/**
+ * Server-side, offline OAuth session-cookie sealing for browser-based Gmail
+ * consent. No Google request, secret persistence, or email delivery.
+ *
+ * MUST NOT be used as a complete OAuth callback: durable one-time replay
+ * prevention and owner authentication are required before production.
+ */
+const SESSION_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
+const CONTEXT = "jaspic-gmail-oauth-v1";
+const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+const decoder = new TextDecoder();
+
+export type GmailWebSession = {
+  state: string;
+  codeVerifier: string;
+  issuedAt: number;
+  expiresAt: number;
+  ownerId?: string;
+};
+
+function decodeBase64Url(value: string): Uint8Array {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(value)) throw new Error("Invalid session cookie");
+  const base = value.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
+  const decoded = atob(base + "=".repeat((4 - base.length % 4) % 4));
+  return Uint8Array.from(decoded, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
+}
+
+function encodeBase64Url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  let s = "";
+  for (const byte of bytes) s += String.fromCharCode(byte);
+  return btoa(s).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");
+}
+
+async function sessionKey(secret: string): Promise<CryptoKey> {
+  // The deployment must provide 32 bytes of key material, encoded as base64url.
+  const bytes = decodeBase64Url(secret);
+  if (bytes.length !== 32) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth session key");
+  return crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", bytes as BufferSource, { name: "AES-GCM" }, false, ["encrypt", "decrypt"]);
+}
+
+function validSession(value: unknown, now: number): value is GmailWebSession {
+  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
+  const s = value as Record<string, unknown>;
+  return typeof s.state === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,100}$/.test(s.state) &&
+    typeof s.codeVerifier === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43,128}$/.test(s.codeVerifier) &&
+    typeof s.issuedAt === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(s.issuedAt) &&
+    typeof s.expiresAt === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(s.expiresAt) &&
+    s.issuedAt <= now && s.expiresAt > now &&
+    s.expiresAt - s.issuedAt <= SESSION_TTL_MS &&
+    (s.ownerId === undefined || (typeof s.ownerId === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$/.test(s.ownerId)));
+}
+
+/** Creates encrypted, short-lived cookie payload; caller sets strict cookie attributes. */
+export async function sealGmailWebSessionOffline(
+  state: string,
+  codeVerifier: string,
+  key: string,
+  now = Date.now(),
+  ownerId?: string,
+): Promise<string> {
+  const session: GmailWebSession = { state, codeVerifier, issuedAt: now, expiresAt: now + SESSION_TTL_MS, ...(ownerId ? {ownerId} : {}) };
+  if (!validSession(session, now)) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth session");
+  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
+  const encoded = encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(session));
+  const ciphertext = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
+    { name: "AES-GCM", iv: iv as BufferSource, additionalData: encoder.encode(CONTEXT) as BufferSource },
+    await sessionKey(key),
+    encoded,
+  );
+  return "v1." + encodeBase64Url(iv) + "." + encodeBase64Url(new Uint8Array(ciphertext));
+}
+
+/** Rejects malformed, tampered, expired and mismatched OAuth state cookies. */
+export async function openGmailWebSessionOffline(
+  cookie: string,
+  expectedState: string,
+  key: string,
+  now = Date.now(),
+): Promise<GmailWebSession> {
+  try {
+    const parts = cookie.split(".");
+    if (parts.length !== 3 || parts[0] !== "v1" || cookie.length > 4096) throw new Error();
+    const iv = decodeBase64Url(parts[1]);
+    const cipher = decodeBase64Url(parts[2]);
+    if (iv.length !== 12 || cipher.length < 16) throw new Error();
+    const result = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
+      { name: "AES-GCM", iv: iv as BufferSource, additionalData: encoder.encode(CONTEXT) as BufferSource },
+      await sessionKey(key),
+      cipher as BufferSource,
+    );
+    const session: unknown = JSON.parse(decoder.decode(result));
+    if (!validSession(session, now) || session.state !== expectedState) throw new Error();
+    return session;
+  } catch {
+    throw new Error("Invalid OAuth session");
+  }
+}
+
+/** Config for server to set alongside OAuth start. Never set from client JS. */
+export const GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY = {
+  name: "__Host-jaspic-oauth",
+  path: "/",
+  httpOnly: true,
+  secure: true,
+  sameSite: "lax",
+  maxAge: 300,
+} as const;

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..eaeaaee7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,50 @@
+/**
+ * Pure orchestration for starting the iPhone Gmail OAuth consent.
+ * This does not launch a browser, call Supabase or Google, or set cookies.
+ * A server-only HTTP adapter must verify the admin's actual session first,
+ * set a Secure HttpOnly cookie and enforce a feature flag.
+ */
+import { authorizeJaspicAdminOffline, type AdminAuthorizationConfig, type TrustedServerIdentity } from "./gmail-oauth-admin-offline.ts";
+import { registerOAuthStateOffline, type OAuthOnceStore } from "./gmail-oauth-once-offline.ts";
+import { prepareGmailWebConsentOffline } from "./gmail-web-oauth-offline.ts";
+import { GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY, sealGmailWebSessionOffline } from "./gmail-web-session-offline.ts";
+
+export type GmailOAuthStartRequest = Readonly<{
+  identity: TrustedServerIdentity | null;
+  adminPolicy: AdminAuthorizationConfig;
+  clientId: string;
+  sessionEncryptionKey: string;
+  stateStore: OAuthOnceStore;
+  now?: number;
+}>;
+
+export type GmailOAuthStartResult = Readonly<{
+  authorizationUrl: string;
+  sealedCookie: string;
+  cookiePolicy: typeof GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY;
+}>;
+
+/**
+ * Register state before disclosing the consent URL. Fails closed if persistent
+ * storage is unavailable; never fall back to process-local memory.
+ */
+export async function prepareAdminGmailOAuthStart(
+  request: GmailOAuthStartRequest,
+): Promise<GmailOAuthStartResult> {
+  const admin = authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(request.identity, request.adminPolicy);
+  const now = request.now ?? Date.now();
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now <= 0) throw new Error("Invalid OAuth start clock");
+  const { state, codeVerifier, authorizationUrl } =
+    await prepareGmailWebConsentOffline(request.clientId);
+  const sealedCookie = await sealGmailWebSessionOffline(
+    state, codeVerifier, request.sessionEncryptionKey, now, admin.ownerId,
+  );
+  await registerOAuthStateOffline(
+    request.stateStore, state, admin.ownerId, now + GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY.maxAge * 1000,
+  );
+  return {
+    authorizationUrl,
+    sealedCookie,
+    cookiePolicy: GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY,
+  };
+}
```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/handler.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/handler.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/handler.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..d76c10cf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/handler.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+import { collect, type Province, type Item } from "./collect.ts";
+import { plan, type Snapshot } from "./state.ts";
+
+export type Store = { load(province: Province): Promise<Snapshot>; commit(province: Province, result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof plan>>): Promise<void>; legacyIds(province: Province): Promise<ReadonlySet<string>> };
+export function createHandler(province: Province, options: {
+  token: string; persistAllowed: boolean; store: Store;
+  collect?: (province: Province) => Promise<Item[]>;
+}) {
+  return async (request: Request): Promise<Response> => {
+    if (request.method !== "POST") return new Response(null, {status:405});
+    if (!options.token) { console.warn("JASPIC_AUTH_REJECTED: token_secret_unavailable"); return new Response(null, {status:401}); }
+    if (request.headers.get("x-jaspic-token") !== options.token) { console.warn("JASPIC_AUTH_REJECTED: token_mismatch_or_missing_header"); return new Response(null, {status:401}); }
+    try {
+      const body = await request.json();
+      // Hard safety barrier: this implementation contains no SMTP, sender API or send mode.
+      if (!body || body.mode !== "dry-run" || (body.persist !== undefined && typeof body.persist !== "boolean")) return new Response(null,{status:400});
+      if (body.persist && !options.persistAllowed) return new Response(null,{status:403});
+      const snapshot = await options.store.load(province);
+      const items = await (options.collect ?? collect)(province);
+      // A large drop may mean valid but incomplete JSON. Require investigation, never auto-rebaseline.
+      for (const kind of new Set(items.map(i => i.kind))) {
+        const oldCount = Object.keys(snapshot.records).filter(k => k.startsWith(kind + ":")).length;
+        if (snapshot.baseline && items.filter(i => i.kind === kind).length < oldCount * 0.8) throw new Error("Source count dropped");
+      }
+      const legacyIds = await options.store.legacyIds(province);
+      const result = await plan(province, snapshot, items, {legacyHiroshimaPersonIds: legacyIds});
+      const candidateBreakdown: Record<string, {new: number; updated: number}> = {};
+      for (const candidate of result.candidates) {
+        const kind = candidate.key.slice(0, candidate.key.indexOf(":"));
+        const counts = candidateBreakdown[kind] ??= {new: 0, updated: 0};
+        counts[snapshot.records[candidate.key] ? "updated" : "new"]++;
+      }
+      const legacyExcluded = province === "hiroshima" && snapshot.baseline
+        ? items.filter(item => item.kind === "hiroshima-person" && legacyIds.has(item.data.id ?? "") &&
+          (!snapshot.records[`hiroshima-person:${item.data.id}`] ||
+          Object.entries(item.data).some(([field, value]) => field !== "latitude" && field !== "longitude" && snapshot.records[`hiroshima-person:${item.data.id}`]?.[field] !== value))).length
+        : 0;
+      if (body.persist) await options.store.commit(province, result);
+      return Response.json({ province, mode:"dry-run", persisted:!!body.persist,
+        baseline:!snapshot.baseline, records:items.length, candidates:result.candidates.length, candidateBreakdown, legacyExcluded, sent:0 });
+    } catch {
+      // Do not expose source text, tokens, HTTP authorization headers, addresses or stack traces.
+      return Response.json({province,mode:"dry-run",sent:0,error:"JASPIC_RUN_FAILED"},{status:503});
+    }
+  };
+}
+
+export function restStore(url: string, key: string, request: typeof fetch = fetch): Store {
+  async function rpc(name: string, args: unknown) {
+    const r = await request(`${url}/rest/v1/rpc/${name}`,{method:"POST",redirect:"error",signal:AbortSignal.timeout(20_000),
+      headers:{apikey:key,Authorization:`Bearer ${key}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},body:JSON.stringify(args)});
+    if (!r.ok) throw new Error("JASPIC store request failed");
+    return r.json();
+  }
+  return {
+    async legacyIds(province) {
+      if (province !== "hiroshima") return new Set<string>();
+      const ids = await rpc("jaspic_legacy_hiroshima_person_ids", {});
+      if (!Array.isArray(ids) || ids.some(id => typeof id !== "string")) throw new Error("Invalid legacy history");
+      return new Set<string>(ids);
+    },
+    async load(province) {
+      const s = await rpc("jaspic_snapshot",{p_province:province});
+      if (!s || !Number.isSafeInteger(s.revision) || typeof s.baseline !== "boolean" || !s.records || typeof s.records !== "object" || Array.isArray(s.records)) throw new Error("Invalid persisted state");
+      return s;
+    },
+    async commit(province,result) {
+      await rpc("jaspic_commit_dry_run",{p_province:province,p_revision:result.expectedRevision,
+        p_records:result.records,p_versions:result.candidates.map(c=>({key:c.key,version:c.version}))});
+    },
+  };
+}

```

```text
+// Port of legacy C# body builders. Transport intentionally absent.
+export type RecordData = Record<string, string | null>;
+export type MailContext = { cityName: string; stationName: string };
+export type Kind = "hiroshima-crime" | "hiroshima-person" | "shiga-person";
+export const fields: Record<Kind, string[]> = {
+  "hiroshima-crime": [
+    "occurrence_date_from",
+    "latitude",
+    "longitude",
+    "id",
+    "city_code",
+    "address",
+    "police_station",
+    "crime_cd",
+    "case_type",
+    "location_type",
+    "damage_goods",
+    "occurrences",
+    "offender",
+    "occurrence_date_to",
+    "date_from_format",
+    "date_to_format"
+  ],
+  "hiroshima-person": [
+    "occurrence_dt",
+    "latitude",
+    "longitude",
+    "id",
+    "city_code",
+    "address",
+    "police_station",
+    "case_type",
+    "crime_cd",
+    "accident_situation",
+    "victim",
+    "criminal_age",
+    "criminal_feature",
+    "resolution",
+    "resolution_info"
+  ],
+  "shiga-person": [
+    "occurrence_dt",
+    "id",
+    "city_code",
+    "address",
+    "address_code",
+    "latitude",
+    "longitude",
+    "police_station",
+    "case_type",
+    "accident_situation",
+    "criminal_car",
+    "criminal_feature",
+    "resolution"
+  ]
+};
+export function month(value: string | null): string {
+  const m = /^(\d{4})[\/-](\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})(?:[ T]|$)/.exec(value ?? "");
+  if (!m || +m[2] < 1 || +m[2] > 12 || +m[3] < 1 || +m[3] > new Date(Date.UTC(+m[1],+m[2],0)).getUTCDate()) throw new Error("Invalid occurrence date");
+  return m[1].slice(2) + "." + m[2].padStart(2,"0");
+}
+export function generateMail(kind: Kind, current: RecordData, db: RecordData | null, context: MailContext) {
+  // C# string concatenation renders null as empty, while comparisons preserve null.
+  const json = new Proxy(current, { get: (target, key: string) => target[key] ?? "" });
+  const city = {city_name: context.cityName};
+  const police_station = {station_name: context.stationName};
+  const feature = {properties: {full_name: context.cityName}};
+  const caseName = ["声かけ","つきまとい","容姿撮影","身体接触","陰部露出","－－－","－－－","－－－","その他"];
+  if (kind === "shiga-person" && (!/^\+?\d+$/.test((current.case_type ?? "").trim()) || Number(current.case_type) < 1 || Number(current.case_type) > 9)) throw new Error("Invalid Shiga category");
+  const marker = (key: string) => db !== null && db[key] !== current[key] ? "●更新" : "";
+  let mailbody = "";
+  switch(kind) {
+    case "hiroshima-crime": {
+      const months = month(current.occurrence_date_from);
+mailbody = "----------------------------------------\r\n";
+            mailbody += "[JASPIC - NES] 広島県警アラート（" + (db === null ? "新規" : "更新") + "）" + (db === null ? "" : "●") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "----------------------------------------\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "広島県警が新しい犯罪情報を公表した可能性があります。\r\n";
+            mailbody += "未配信情報の場合、県警サイトを確認して対応をお願いします。\r\n";
+            mailbody += "https://map.police.hiroshima.dsvc.jp/?lat=" + json.latitude + "&lon=" + json.longitude + "&zoom=14&mode=0&period=1&months=" + months + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "{\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"id\":\"" + json.id + "\",　→管理番号" + marker("id") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"city_code\":\"" + json.city_code + "\",　→" + city.city_name + marker("city_code") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"address\":\"" + json.address + "\",　→町域" + marker("address") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"latitude\":\"" + json.latitude + "\",　→緯度（参考程度）" + marker("latitude") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"longitude\":\"" + json.longitude + "\",　→経度（参考程度）" + marker("longitude") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"police_station\":\"" + json.police_station + "\",　→" + police_station.station_name + (!police_station.station_name.includes("警察署") ? "警察署" : "") + marker("police_station") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"crime_cd\":\"" + json.crime_cd + "\",　→不明" + marker("crime_cd") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"case_type\":\"" + json.case_type + "\",　→カテゴリ" + marker("case_type") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"location_type\":\"" + json.location_type + "\",　→発生場所かな？" + marker("location_type") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"damage_goods\":\"" + json.damage_goods + "\",　→被害品かな？" + marker("damage_goods") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"occurrences\":\"" + json.occurrences + "\",　→被害品" + marker("occurrences") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"offender\":\"" + json.offender + "\",　→加害者" + marker("offender") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"occurrence_date_from\":\"" + json.occurrence_date_from + "\",　→発生日時（から）" + marker("occurrence_date_from") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"occurrence_date_to\":\"" + json.occurrence_date_to + "\",　→発生日時（まで）" + marker("occurrence_date_to") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"date_from_format\":\"" + json.date_from_format + "\",　→不明" + marker("date_from_format") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"date_to_format\":\"" + json.date_to_format + "\" →不明" + marker("date_to_format") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "}\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "---\r\n";
+            mailbody += "JASPIC - NES（広島県警専用ツール）\r\n";
+            mailbody += "↓ウェブサイト掲載情報とURLをここにコピペしてください↓\r\n";
+            mailbody += "---\r\n";
+      break;
+    }
+    case "hiroshima-person": {
+      const months = month(current.occurrence_dt);
+mailbody = "----------------------------------------\r\n";
+            mailbody += "[JASPIC - NES] 広島県警アラート（" + (db === null ? "新規" : "更新") + "）" + (db === null ? "" : "●") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "----------------------------------------\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "広島県警が新しい不審者情報を公表した可能性があります。\r\n";
+            mailbody += "未配信情報の場合、県警サイトを確認して対応をお願いします。\r\n";
+            mailbody += "https://map.police.hiroshima.dsvc.jp/?lat=" + json.latitude + "&lon=" + json.longitude + "&zoom=14&mode=1&period=1&months=" + months + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "{\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"id\":\"" + json.id + "\",　→管理番号" + marker("id") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"city_code\":\"" + json.city_code + "\",　→" + city.city_name + marker("city_code") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"address\":\"" + json.address + "\",　→発生場所" + marker("address") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"latitude\":\"" + json.latitude + "\",　→緯度（参考程度）" + marker("latitude") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"longitude\":\"" + json.longitude + "\",　→経度（参考程度）" + marker("longitude") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"police_station\":\"" + json.police_station + "\",　→" + police_station.station_name + (!police_station.station_name.includes("警察署") ? "警察署" : "") + marker("police_station") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"case_type\":\"" + json.case_type + "\",　→カテゴリ" + marker("case_type") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"crime_cd\":\"" + json.crime_cd + "\",　→不明" + marker("crime_cd") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"occurrence_dt\":\"" + json.occurrence_dt + "\",　→発生日時" + marker("occurrence_dt") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"accident_situation\":\"" + json.accident_situation + "\",　→事案概要" + marker("accident_situation") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"victim\":\"" + json.victim + "\",　→被害者" + marker("victim") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"criminal_age\":\"" + json.criminal_age + "\",　→加害者年齢" + marker("criminal_age") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"criminal_feature\":\"" + json.criminal_feature + "\",　→加害者特徴" + marker("criminal_feature") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"resolution\":\"" + (json.resolution == "1" ? "検挙" : "") + (json.resolution == "2" ? "指導・警告" : "") + (json.resolution == "9" ? "その他" : "") + "\",　→解決" + marker("resolution") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"resolution_info\":\"" + json.resolution_info + "\" →不明" + marker("resolution_info") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "}\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "---\r\n";
+            mailbody += "JASPIC - NES（広島県警専用ツール）\r\n";
+            mailbody += "↓ウェブサイト掲載情報とURLをここにコピペしてください↓\r\n";
+            mailbody += "---\r\n";
+      break;
+    }
+    case "shiga-person": {
+      const months = month(current.occurrence_dt);
+mailbody = "----------------------------------------\r\n";
+            mailbody += "[JASPIC - NES] 滋賀県警アラート（" + (db === null ? "新規" : "更新") + "）" + (db === null ? "" : "●") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "----------------------------------------\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "滋賀県警が新しい不審者情報を公表した可能性があります。\r\n";
+            mailbody += "https://map.police.shiga.dsvc.jp\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "{\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"id\":\"" + json.id + "\"　→管理番号" + marker("id") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"city_code\":\"" + json.city_code + "\"　→" + feature.properties.full_name + marker("city_code") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"address\":\"" + json.address + "\"　→発生場所" + marker("address") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"address_code\":\"" + json.address_code + "\"　→発生場所" + marker("address_code") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"latitude\":\"" + json.latitude + "\"　→緯度（参考程度）" + marker("latitude") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"longitude\":\"" + json.longitude + "\"　→経度（参考程度）" + marker("longitude") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"police_station\":\"" + json.police_station + "\"　→管轄警察署" + marker("police_station") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"case_type\":\"" + json.case_type + "\"　→" + caseName[Number(json.case_type) - 1] + marker("case_type") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"occurrence_dt\":\"" + json.occurrence_dt + "\"　→発生日時" + marker("occurrence_dt") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"accident_situation\":\"" + json.accident_situation + "\"　→発生状況" + marker("accident_situation") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"criminal_car\":\"" + json.criminal_car + "\"　→不審者の移動手段" + marker("criminal_car") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"criminal_feature\":\"" + json.criminal_feature + "\"　→不審者の特徴" + marker("criminal_feature") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\"resolution\":\"" + json.resolution + "\"　→" + (json.resolution == "0" ? "未解決" : "解決済み") + marker("resolution") + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "}\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "データ中の緯度経度をグーグルマップで開く\r\n";
+            mailbody += "https://www.google.com/maps?q=" + json.latitude + "," + json.longitude + "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "\r\n";
+            mailbody += "---\r\n";
+            mailbody += "JASPIC - NES（滋賀県警専用ツール）\r\n";
+            mailbody += "↓ウェブサイト掲載情報とURLをここにコピペしてください↓\r\n";
+            mailbody += "---\r\n";
+            mailbody += "https://map.police.shiga.dsvc.jp\r\n";
+      break;
+    }
+  }
+  const province = kind === "shiga-person" ? "滋賀" : "広島";
+  return { from: "hppmap@kishibojim.online", to: "getter01@jaspic-nes.net",
+    cc: kind === "shiga-person" ? "kishibojim@gmail.com" : "miyazaki1016@me.com",
+    subject: " [JASPIC-NES] " + province + "県警専用ツール", body: mailbody };
+}
```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..ee036430
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+import { generateMail, type Kind, type RecordData, type MailContext } from "./mail.ts";
+
+export type MimeMode = "unprepared" | "seven-bit" | "eight-bit";
+export type MimeIdentity = { date: string; messageId: string };
+const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
+function base64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  let result = "";
+  for (let i=0;i<bytes.length;i+=3) {
+    const n=(bytes[i]<<16)|((bytes[i+1]??0)<<8)|(bytes[i+2]??0);
+    result+=alphabet[n>>>18]+alphabet[(n>>>12)&63]+(i+1<bytes.length?alphabet[(n>>>6)&63]:"=")+(i+2<bytes.length?alphabet[n&63]:"=");
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+/** Offline, fixed JASPIC text/plain profile. No network, SMTP or secret handling. */
+export function generateEml(kind: Kind, current: RecordData, previous: RecordData | null, context: MailContext,
+  mode: MimeMode, identity: MimeIdentity): Uint8Array {
+  if (!["unprepared","seven-bit","eight-bit"].includes(mode)) throw new Error("Unsupported MIME mode");
+  if (!/^[\x20-\x7e]+$/.test(identity.date) || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.@-]+$/.test(identity.messageId)) throw new Error("Invalid MIME identity");
+  const mail=generateMail(kind,current,previous,context);
+  if (mail.body.replaceAll("\r\n","").match(/[\r\n]/)) throw new Error("Body must use CRLF");
+  const bodyBytes=new TextEncoder().encode(mail.body);
+  if (bodyBytes.some(b=>b===0) || mail.body.split("\r\n").some(line=>new TextEncoder().encode(line).length>998)) throw new Error("MIME profile line limit exceeded");
+  if(mode!=="unprepared" && /(^|\r\n)From /.test(mail.body)) throw new Error("Quoted-printable profile not yet verified");
+  // MimeKit 4.12 strips leading Subject whitespace while RFC2047 encoding the Japanese word.
+  // The logical Subject in mail.ts remains unchanged, including its original leading space.
+  const japaneseSubject=(kind==="shiga-person"?"滋賀":"広島")+"県警専用ツール";
+  const headers=[`From: ${mail.from}`,`Date: ${identity.date}`,
+    `Subject: [JASPIC-NES] =?utf-8?b?${base64(new TextEncoder().encode(japaneseSubject))}?=`,
+    `Message-Id: <${identity.messageId}>`,`To: ${mail.to}`,`Cc: ${mail.cc}`,"MIME-Version: 1.0","Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8"];
+  let body=mail.body;
+  if(mode==="eight-bit") headers.push("Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit");
+  if(mode==="seven-bit") {
+    // MimeKit 4.12 BestEncodingFilter: count8 >= (int)(total * 0.17).
+    // Fail closed for QP rather than guessing its byte wrapping.
+    const high=bodyBytes.filter(b=>b>127).length;
+    if(high<Math.floor(bodyBytes.length*0.17)) throw new Error("Quoted-printable profile not yet verified");
+    headers.push("Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64");
+    body=base64(bodyBytes).match(/.{1,76}/g)!.join("\r\n")+"\r\n";
+  }
+  return new TextEncoder().encode(headers.join("\r\n")+"\r\n\r\n"+body);
+}

```

### supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state.ts

```text
diff --git a/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state.ts b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..4b8795f1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import { fields, type Kind, type RecordData, generateMail, month } from "./mail.ts";
+import type { Item, Province } from "./collect.ts";
+
+export type Snapshot = { revision: number; baseline: boolean; records: Record<string, RecordData> };
+export type Candidate = { key: string; version: string; mail: ReturnType<typeof generateMail> };
+export const keyOf = (kind: Kind, id: string) => `${kind}:${id}`;
+export async function digest(value: unknown): Promise<string> {
+  const bytes = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(value)));
+  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(bytes), b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
+}
+const compareFields = (kind: Kind) => fields[kind].filter(f => f !== "latitude" && f !== "longitude");
+function day(value: string | null): string {
+  month(value); // validates the calendar date without timezone conversion
+  const match = /^(\d{4})[\/-](\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})/.exec(value!)!;
+  return `${match[1]}-${match[2].padStart(2,"0")}-${match[3].padStart(2,"0")}`;
+}
+function eligible(item: Item, previous: RecordData | null, all: Record<string, RecordData>): boolean {
+  const d = item.data;
+  if (item.kind === "shiga-person") return day(d.occurrence_dt) >= "2024-01-01";
+  if (d.case_type === "自転車盗") return false;
+  if (item.kind === "hiroshima-person") return true;
+  if (day(d.occurrence_date_from) < "2023-06-01") return false;
+  // Legacy criminal_chk applies only to new IDs, before inserting each record.
+  return previous !== null || !Object.entries(all).some(([key, old]) => key.startsWith("hiroshima-crime:") &&
+    old.occurrence_date_from === d.occurrence_date_from && old.occurrence_date_to === d.occurrence_date_to && d.id!.includes(old.id!));
+}
+export async function plan(province: Province, snapshot: Snapshot, items: Item[], options: {legacyHiroshimaPersonIds?: ReadonlySet<string>} = {}) {
+  if (!items.length || items.some(i => !i.kind.startsWith(province))) throw new Error("Incomplete or mixed snapshot");
+  const records = { ...snapshot.records }; const candidates: Candidate[] = []; const seen = new Set<string>();
+  // Unknown removed records remain tombstones: reappearing historical IDs are never new.
+  for (const item of items) {
+    if (!item.data.id) throw new Error("Missing ID");
+    const key = keyOf(item.kind, item.data.id);
+    if (seen.has(key)) throw new Error("Duplicate ID"); seen.add(key);
+    const previous = records[key] ?? null;
+    const changed = previous === null || compareFields(item.kind).some(f => previous[f] !== item.data[f]);
+    if (!snapshot.baseline || changed) {
+      const legacyExcluded = item.kind === "hiroshima-person" && options.legacyHiroshimaPersonIds?.has(item.data.id);
+      if (snapshot.baseline && !legacyExcluded && eligible(item, previous, records)) {
+        const version = await digest(compareFields(item.kind).map(f => [f, item.data[f]]));
+        candidates.push({ key, version, mail: generateMail(item.kind, item.data, previous, item.context) });
+      }
+      records[key] = item.data;
+    }
+    // Coordinate-only changes intentionally do not update stored values, matching C#.
+  }
+  return { expectedRevision: snapshot.revision, records, candidates, baseline: true as const };
+}
+
+export type DeliveryStatus = "PREPARED" | "SENDING" | "SENT" | "UNKNOWN" | "CANCELLED";
+export function transition(status: DeliveryStatus, event: "claim" | "accepted" | "uncertain" | "cancel"): DeliveryStatus {
+  if (event === "claim" && status === "PREPARED") return "SENDING";
+  if (event === "accepted" && status === "SENDING") return "SENT";
+  if (event === "uncertain" && status === "SENDING") return "UNKNOWN";
+  if (event === "cancel" && status === "PREPARED") return "CANCELLED";
+  throw new Error("Unsafe delivery transition");
+}

```

### supabase/migrations/20261105074100_jaspic_oauth_once.sql

```text
diff --git a/supabase/migrations/20261105074100_jaspic_oauth_once.sql b/supabase/migrations/20261105074100_jaspic_oauth_once.sql
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..55f6e8cf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/supabase/migrations/20261105074100_jaspic_oauth_once.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+-- JASPIC Gmail OAuth: single-use state/owner gate (NOT APPLIED TO PRODUCTION)
+-- Review-only migration in Draft PR #155; no mail sending or credentials.
+-- This is not a replacement for server-side admin authentication.
+create table if not exists public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states (
+  state_hash text primary key
+    check (state_hash ~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$'),
+  owner_id text not null
+    check (owner_id ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$'),
+  expires_at timestamptz not null,
+  consumed_at timestamptz,
+  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
+  constraint jaspic_oauth_state_expiry_after_create check (expires_at > created_at)
+);
+
+comment on table public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states is
+  'JASPIC OAuth one-time CSRF state digests only. No token, code, PKCE verifier, or email address.';
+
+alter table public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states enable row level security;
+revoke all on table public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states from public, anon, authenticated;
+grant select, insert, update, delete on table public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states to service_role;
+
+-- Atomic consume: only one concurrent caller can change consumed_at from NULL.
+-- Return bool, not secret state; use only from authenticated server-side flow.
+create or replace function public.jaspic_consume_gmail_oauth_state(
+  p_state_hash text,
+  p_owner_id text
+)
+returns boolean
+language plpgsql
+security invoker
+set search_path = ''
+as $$
+declare
+  v_consumed boolean := false;
+begin
+  if p_state_hash !~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$'
+     or p_owner_id !~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,128}$' then
+    return false;
+  end if;
+
+  update public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states
+     set consumed_at = now()
+   where state_hash = p_state_hash
+     and owner_id = p_owner_id
+     and consumed_at is null
+     and expires_at > now()
+  returning true into v_consumed;
+
+  return coalesce(v_consumed, false);
+end;
+$$;
+
+revoke all on function public.jaspic_consume_gmail_oauth_state(text,text) from public, anon, authenticated;
+grant execute on function public.jaspic_consume_gmail_oauth_state(text,text) to service_role;
+
+-- Optional maintenance only after review/approval: delete expired entries
+-- without retaining OAuth state indefinitely. Do not schedule automatically.
+-- delete from public.jaspic_gmail_oauth_states where expires_at < now() - interval '1 day';

```

### tests/jaspic/concurrency.py

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/concurrency.py b/tests/jaspic/concurrency.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..c91eaab1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/concurrency.py
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+"""Disposable Postgres race tests. Uses only CI PG* environment variables."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import subprocess
+
+def sql(query):
+    return subprocess.run(['psql','-X','-A','-t','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-c',query],capture_output=True,text=True)
+
+def race(queries):
+    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+        return list(pool.map(sql,queries))
+
+snapshot="'{\"shiga-person:RACE\":{\"id\":\"RACE\"}}'::jsonb"
+results=race([f"select public.jaspic_commit_dry_run('shiga',0,{snapshot},'[]');"]*2)
+assert sum(r.returncode==0 for r in results)==1, 'CAS must admit exactly one baseline'
+version='b'*64
+assert sql(f"insert into public.jaspic_deliveries(province,record_key,version,status) values ('shiga','shiga-person:RACE','{version}','PREPARED');").returncode==0
+results=race([f"select public.jaspic_claim('shiga','shiga-person:RACE','{version}','00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000{i}');" for i in (1,2)])
+assert all(r.returncode==0 for r in results)
+assert sorted(r.stdout.strip() for r in results)==['f','t'], 'Only one worker may own a delivery'
+print('Independent-province CAS and delivery claim races passed')

```

### tests/jaspic/database.sql

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/database.sql b/tests/jaspic/database.sql
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..d424075a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/database.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+-- Disposable database ONLY; no external requests or production credentials.
+create role anon nologin;
+create role authenticated nologin;
+create role service_role nologin bypassrls;
+\ir ../../docs/sql/jaspic-isolated-draft.sql
+do $$
+declare result bigint; v text := repeat('a',64); k text := 'hiroshima-crime:TEST'; token uuid := '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001';
+begin
+  if has_table_privilege('anon','public.jaspic_records','SELECT') or has_table_privilege('authenticated','public.jaspic_deliveries','INSERT') then raise exception 'public access'; end if;
+  if has_function_privilege('anon','public.jaspic_commit_dry_run(text,bigint,jsonb,jsonb)','EXECUTE') then raise exception 'public RPC'; end if;
+  if exists(select 1 from pg_class where relname in ('jaspic_sources','jaspic_records','jaspic_deliveries') and not relrowsecurity) then raise exception 'RLS disabled'; end if;
+  begin
+    perform public.jaspic_commit_dry_run('hiroshima',0,'{}','[]');
+    raise exception 'empty baseline accepted';
+  exception when raise_exception then if SQLERRM = 'empty baseline accepted' then raise; end if; end;
+  result := public.jaspic_commit_dry_run('hiroshima',0,jsonb_build_object(k,jsonb_build_object('id','TEST')),'[]');
+  if result <> 1 or (select count(*) from public.jaspic_deliveries) <> 0 then raise exception 'baseline not silent'; end if;
+  begin
+    perform public.jaspic_commit_dry_run('hiroshima',0,jsonb_build_object(k,jsonb_build_object('id','TEST')),'[]');
+    raise exception 'stale write accepted';
+  exception when raise_exception then if SQLERRM = 'stale write accepted' then raise; end if; end;
+  perform public.jaspic_commit_dry_run('hiroshima',1,jsonb_build_object(k,jsonb_build_object('id','TEST')),jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('key',k,'version',v)));
+  perform public.jaspic_commit_dry_run('hiroshima',2,jsonb_build_object(k,jsonb_build_object('id','TEST')),jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('key',k,'version',v)));
+  if (select count(*) from public.jaspic_deliveries) <> 1 then raise exception 'duplicate version'; end if;
+  if public.jaspic_claim('hiroshima',k,v,token) then raise exception 'claimed dry-run'; end if;
+  update public.jaspic_deliveries set status='PREPARED';
+  if not public.jaspic_claim('hiroshima',k,v,token) then raise exception 'claim failed'; end if;
+  if public.jaspic_claim('hiroshima',k,v,token) then raise exception 'double claim'; end if;
+  if public.jaspic_finish('hiroshima',k,v,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002',true) then raise exception 'stolen lease'; end if;
+  if not public.jaspic_finish('hiroshima',k,v,token,false) then raise exception 'unknown finish failed'; end if;
+  if public.jaspic_claim('hiroshima',k,v,token) then raise exception 'unknown retry'; end if;
+  update public.jaspic_deliveries set status='SENDING',lease_until=now()-interval '1 second';
+  if public.jaspic_expire_uncertain('hiroshima') <> 1 then raise exception 'expiry failed'; end if;
+  if public.jaspic_claim('hiroshima',k,v,token) then raise exception 'expired retry'; end if;
+  if (public.jaspic_snapshot('shiga')->>'baseline')::boolean then raise exception 'province contamination'; end if;
+end;
+$$;
+-- A legitimate service role can use the RPC, while normal clients have no API grants.
+set role service_role;
+select public.jaspic_snapshot('hiroshima');
+reset role;

```

### tests/jaspic/eml.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/eml.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/eml.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3fb65693
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/eml.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+// @ts-expect-error Node-only diagnostic script intentionally has no TS declaration.
+import { compareEml } from "../../scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs";
+const message=Buffer.from('From: hppmap@kishibojim.online\r\nTo: getter01@jaspic-nes.net\r\nCc: kishibojim@gmail.com\r\nSubject:  [JASPIC-NES] test\r\nMIME-Version: 1.0\r\nContent-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8\r\nContent-Transfer-Encoding: base64\r\nDate: Wed, 4 Nov 2026 00:00:00 +0000\r\nMessage-ID: <synthetic@localhost>\r\n\r\neA0K\r\n');
+it("MIME comparator detects subject space, charset, CTE, folding and body mismatches",()=>{
+  for(const [from,to] of [['Subject:  ','Subject: '],['charset=utf-8','charset=iso-2022-jp'],['base64','quoted-printable'],['eA0K','eQ0K'],['Cc: kishibojim','Cc:\r\n kishibojim']]) {
+    expect(compareEml(message,Buffer.from(message.toString().replace(from,to)))).toBe(false);
+  }
+  expect(compareEml(message,Buffer.from(message.toString().replace('Wed, 4 Nov','Thu, 5 Nov')))).toBe(true);
+  expect(()=>compareEml(message,Buffer.from(message.toString().replaceAll('\r\n','\n')))).toThrow();
+});
+it("requires CTE after preparation, and preserves its absence before preparation",()=>{
+  const unprepared=Buffer.from(message.toString().replace('Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64\r\n',''));
+  expect(()=>compareEml(unprepared,unprepared)).toThrow();
+  expect(compareEml(unprepared,unprepared,{stage:'unprepared'})).toBe(true);
+  expect(compareEml(unprepared,message,{stage:'unprepared'})).toBe(false);
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..266ab1ff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { prepareGmailSendRequestOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline";
+
+describe("Gmail API send descriptor (offline only)", () => {
+  it("prepares the documented users.me.messages.send request without sending", () => {
+    const request = prepareGmailSendRequestOffline("ZmFrZV9lbWw", "synthetic-token");
+    expect(request.url).toBe("https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/send");
+    expect(request.method).toBe("POST");
+    expect(request.headers).toEqual({
+      Authorization: "[REDACTED]", "Content-Type": "application/json",
+    });
+    expect(JSON.parse(request.body)).toEqual({ raw: "ZmFrZV9lbWw" });
+  });
+  it("fails closed for invalid raw or token without leaking token", () => {
+    const token = "synthetic-private-token";
+    for (const badRaw of ["", "not/base64", "a=b", "a\r\nInjected: yes"]) {
+      expect(() => prepareGmailSendRequestOffline(badRaw, token)).toThrow("Invalid encoded MIME payload");
+    }
+    for (const badToken of ["", "injected\r\nHeader: value", "contains space"]) {
+      expect(() => prepareGmailSendRequestOffline("ZmFrZQ", badToken)).toThrow("Invalid OAuth access token");
+    }
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..02150380
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  GMAIL_SEND_SCOPE, prepareRefreshRequestOffline,
+  validateRefreshResponseOffline, nextDeliveryState,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-auth-offline";
+
+describe("Gmail OAuth offline-only preparation", () => {
+  it("builds a form request without executing any network operation", () => {
+    const request = prepareRefreshRequestOffline({
+      clientId: "test-client", [REDACTED], [REDACTED],
+    });
+    expect(request.method).toBe("POST");
+    expect(request.url).toBe("https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token");
+    expect(request.headers["Content-Type"]).toBe("application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
+    const form = new URLSearchParams(request.body);
+    expect(form.get("grant_type")).toBe("refresh_token");
+    expect(form.get("client_id")).toBe("test-client");
+    expect(form.get("client_secret")).toBe("fake-only-secret");
+    expect(form.get("refresh_token")).toBe("fake-only-token");
+    expect(GMAIL_SEND_SCOPE).toBe("https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send");
+  });
+  it("refuses invalid credentials without echoing them", () => {
+    for (const bad of ["", " x", "x\r\nInjected: y"]) {
+      expect(() => prepareRefreshRequestOffline({
+        clientId: "client", clientSecret: bad, refreshToken: "dummy",
+      })).toThrow("Invalid OAuth configuration");
+    }
+  });
+  it("validates token responses and fails closed without disclosing tokens", () => {
+    expect(validateRefreshResponseOffline({
+      [REDACTED], token_type: "Bearer", expires_in: 3599,
+    })).toEqual({ [REDACTED], tokenType: "Bearer", expiresInSeconds: 3599 });
+    for (const bad of [
+      null, {}, { error: "invalid_grant", error_description: "private-secret" },
+      { [REDACTED], token_type: "mac", expires_in: 3000 },
+      { [REDACTED], token_type: "Bearer", expires_in: 0 },
+    ]) {
+      try {
+        validateRefreshResponseOffline(bad);
+        throw new Error("Expected rejection");
+      } catch (e) {
+        expect(String(e)).not.toContain("private-secret");
+        expect(String(e)).toContain("Invalid OAuth response");
+      }
+    }
+  });
+  it("guards against automatic retry after unknown delivery", () => {
+    expect(nextDeliveryState("NEW", "lease")).toBe("LEASED");
+    expect(nextDeliveryState("LEASED", "accepted")).toBe("ACCEPTED");
+    expect(nextDeliveryState("LEASED", "unknown")).toBe("UNKNOWN");
+    expect(nextDeliveryState("LEASED", "failed")).toBe("FAILED");
+    for (const state of ["ACCEPTED", "UNKNOWN", "FAILED"] as const) {
+      expect(() => nextDeliveryState(state, "lease")).toThrow();
+    }
+    expect(() => nextDeliveryState("NEW", "accepted")).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..17b0d113
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-dry-run.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import fixtures from "./mime-fixtures.json";
+import { generateEml } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime";
+import { JASPIC_GMAIL_FROM, prepareGmailRawOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-dry-run";
+import type { Kind, RecordData } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail";
+
+const identity = { date: "Wed, 04 Nov 2026 18:00:00 +0900", messageId: "test@localhost.invalid" };
+describe("offline Gmail API envelope preparation (no network)", () => {
+  for (const f of fixtures) {
+    it(f.kind + (f.previous === null ? "-new" : "-update"), () => {
+      const original = Buffer.from(generateEml(f.kind as Kind, f.current as RecordData,
+        f.previous as RecordData | null, f, "eight-bit", identity));
+      const result = prepareGmailRawOffline(original);
+      const base64 = result.raw.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
+      const output = Buffer.from(base64, "base64").toString("utf8");
+      const oldText = original.toString("utf8");
+      const divider = "\r\n\r\n";
+      expect(result.from).toBe(JASPIC_GMAIL_FROM);
+      expect(output).toContain("From: miyazaki1016@gmail.com\r\n");
+      expect(output.slice(output.indexOf(divider) + divider.length)).toBe(
+        oldText.slice(oldText.indexOf(divider) + divider.length));
+      expect(output.replace("From: miyazaki1016@gmail.com", "From: hppmap@kishibojim.online"))
+        .toBe(oldText);
+      expect(result.raw).toMatch(/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/);
+    });
+  }
+  it("refuses unexpected From, added routing header, and broken MIME", () => {
+    const f = fixtures[0];
+    const src = Buffer.from(generateEml(f.kind as Kind, f.current as RecordData,
+      f.previous as RecordData | null, f, "eight-bit", identity)).toString("utf8");
+    for (const bad of [
+      src.replace("From: hppmap@kishibojim.online", "From: attacker@example.org"),
+      src.replace("From:", "Return-Path: spoof@example.org\r\nFrom:"),
+      src.replace("\r\n\r\n", "\n\n"),
+    ]) expect(() => prepareGmailRawOffline(new TextEncoder().encode(bad))).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-google-sender-identity-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-google-sender-identity-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-google-sender-identity-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..01f7c871
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-google-sender-identity-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { GOOGLE_USERINFO_ENDPOINT, validateGoogleSenderIdentityOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-google-sender-identity-offline";
+
+describe("Google sender identity is checked before token persistence (offline only)", () => {
+  const expected = "owner@example.com";
+  const valid = { sub: "123456789012345", email: "owner@example.com", email_verified: true };
+  it("accepts only Google's verified matching email and stable subject", () => {
+    expect(GOOGLE_USERINFO_ENDPOINT).toBe("https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo");
+    expect(validateGoogleSenderIdentityOffline(valid, expected)).toEqual({
+      googleSubject: valid.sub, verifiedEmail: expected,
+    });
+    expect(validateGoogleSenderIdentityOffline({ ...valid, email: "OWNER@EXAMPLE.COM" }, expected).googleSubject).toBe(valid.sub);
+  });
+  it("rejects a different Google account, unverified email and malformed responses", () => {
+    for (const response of [
+      null, [], {}, { ...valid, email: "attacker@example.com" },
+      { ...valid, email_verified: false }, { ...valid, email_verified: "true" },
+      { ...valid, sub: null }, { ...valid, sub: "" }, { ...valid, sub: "spoofed" },
+      { ...valid, email: "owner@example.com " },
+    ]) {
+      expect(() => validateGoogleSenderIdentityOffline(response, expected)).toThrow();
+    }
+    expect(() => validateGoogleSenderIdentityOffline(valid, "bad-email")).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline.test.ts

```text

new file mode 100644

+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  authorizeJaspicAdminOffline, type TrustedServerIdentity,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline";
+
+const config = {
+  expectedSubject: "owner_subject_1234",
+  expectedEmail: "owner@example.invalid",
+  expectedIssuer: "https://auth.example.invalid",
+};
+const identity: TrustedServerIdentity = {
+  subject: config.expectedSubject,
+  verifiedEmail: config.expectedEmail,
+  issuer: config.expectedIssuer,
+  sessionVerified: true,
+};
+
+describe("offline JASPIC Gmail admin access policy", () => {
+  it("grants only the explicitly configured owner after trusted verification", () => {
+    expect(authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(identity, config)).toEqual({
+      ownerId: "owner_subject_1234",
+    });
+  });
+
+  it("denies missing, mismatched or unverified identities", () => {
+    const candidates = [
+      null,
+      {...identity, subject: "different_subject_1234"},
+      {...identity, verifiedEmail: "attacker@example.invalid"},
+      {...identity, issuer: "https://other.example.invalid"},
+      {...identity, sessionVerified: false},
+      {...identity, subject: ""},
+    ];
+    for (const candidate of candidates) {
+      expect(() => authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(
+        candidate as TrustedServerIdentity | null, config,
+      )).toThrow("JASPIC admin access denied");
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("fails closed if configured owner fields are incomplete", () => {
+    expect(() => authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(identity, {
+      ...config, expectedSubject: "",
+    })).toThrow("JASPIC admin access denied");
+  });
+});
```
