```text
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+
+const sql = readFileSync(
+  new URL("../../supabase/migrations/20261105074100_jaspic_oauth_once.sql", import.meta.url),
+  "utf8",
+);
+
+describe("JASPIC OAuth atomic state SQL review gate (not deployed)", () => {
+  it("enforces state hash uniqueness and owner-bound expiry", () => {
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/state_hash text primary key/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/owner_id text not null/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/consumed_at timestamptz/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/expires_at > now\(\)/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/owner_id = p_owner_id/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/consumed_at is null/);
+  });
+  it("restricts table and RPC access to server-side service role", () => {
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/enable row level security/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/revoke all on table .* from public, anon, authenticated/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/grant .* to service_role/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/security invoker/);
+    expect(sql).toMatch(/revoke all on function .* from public, anon, authenticated/);
+  });
+  it("does not apply scheduled jobs or send mail", () => {
+    expect(sql).not.toMatch(/cron\.schedule\s*\(/);
+    expect(sql).not.toMatch(/gmail\.send\s*\(/);
+  });
+});
```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..8d21a726
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  consumeOAuthStateOffline, hashGmailOAuthStateOffline,
+  registerOAuthStateOffline, type OAuthOnceStore, type OAuthOnceChallenge,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline";
+
+function syntheticAtomicStore(): OAuthOnceStore {
+  const table = new Map<string, OAuthOnceChallenge>();
+  return {
+    async create(input) {
+      if (table.has(input.stateHash)) throw new Error("Duplicate OAuth state");
+      table.set(input.stateHash, input);
+    },
+    async consumeAtomically(input) {
+      const record = table.get(input.stateHash);
+      if (!record || record.ownerId !== input.ownerId || record.expiresAt <= input.now) {
+        return "invalid_or_expired";
+      }
+      table.delete(input.stateHash);
+      return "consumed";
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+describe("OAuth state single use offline contract", () => {
+  const state = "s".repeat(43);
+  const owner = "syntheticOwner123";
+  const now = 1_800_000_000_000;
+  it("hashes state before persistence, uses a one-time consume contract", async () => {
+    const store = syntheticAtomicStore();
+    const hash = await hashGmailOAuthStateOffline(state);
+    expect(hash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
+    expect(hash).not.toContain(state);
+    await registerOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now + 60_000);
+    expect(await consumeOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now)).toBe(true);
+    expect(await consumeOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now)).toBe(false);
+  });
+  it("models atomic consumption where concurrent callbacks have one winner", async () => {
+    const store = syntheticAtomicStore();
+    await registerOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now + 60_000);
+    const results = await Promise.all(Array.from({length: 16}, () =>
+      consumeOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now)));
+    expect(results.filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+  it("rejects wrong owner, expired session and invalid state", async () => {
+    const store = syntheticAtomicStore();
+    await registerOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now + 10);
+    expect(await consumeOAuthStateOffline(store, state, "differentOwner12", now)).toBe(false);
+    expect(await consumeOAuthStateOffline(store, state, owner, now + 11)).toBe(false);
+    await expect(registerOAuthStateOffline(store, "bad", owner, now + 100)).rejects.toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3ba82193
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,51 @@
+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import {
+  verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity, type SupabaseAuthVerifier,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-auth";
+import { authorizeJaspicAdminOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-admin-offline";
+
+const issuer = "https://test-project.supabase.co/auth/v1";
+const id = "12345678-abcd-4def-9123-000000000001";
+const email = "owner@example.invalid";
+const confirmed = "2026-11-05T00:00:00.000Z";
+
+function verifier(user: {id: string;email: string|null;email_confirmed_at: string|null}|null): SupabaseAuthVerifier {
+  return {auth: {getUser: vi.fn(async () => ({data: {user},error: null}))}};
+}
+
+describe("JASPIC Supabase Auth adapter (mock-only)", () => {
+  it("reads user from server-side getUser and checks verified owner", async () => {
+    const auth = verifier({id,email,email_confirmed_at: confirmed});
+    const user = await verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(auth, "mock-access-token", {issuer});
+    expect(auth.auth.getUser).toHaveBeenCalledWith("mock-access-token");
+    expect(authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(user,{
+      expectedSubject: id, expectedEmail: email, expectedIssuer: issuer,
+    })).toEqual({ownerId: id});
+  });
+
+  it("rejects missing confirmation, missing user and invalid issuer", async () => {
+    await expect(verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(verifier({id,email,email_confirmed_at: null}),
+      "mock-access-token", {issuer})).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC login required");
+    await expect(verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(verifier(null),
+      "mock-access-token", {issuer})).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC login required");
+    await expect(verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(verifier({id,email,email_confirmed_at: confirmed}),
+      "mock-access-token", {issuer: "http://bad.example"})).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC login required");
+  });
+
+  it("rejects Auth verification failures without revealing server details", async () => {
+    const auth: SupabaseAuthVerifier = {auth: {getUser: async () => ({
+      data: {user: null}, error: {message: "internal-auth-error"},
+    })}};
+    await expect(verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(auth,"mock-access-token",{issuer}))
+      .rejects.toThrow("JASPIC login required");
+  });
+
+  it("never accepts caller-provided email or subject as verification", async () => {
+    const auth = verifier({id: "12345678-abcd-4def-9123-000000000002",email,
+      email_confirmed_at: confirmed});
+    const user = await verifyJaspicSupabaseIdentity(auth,"mock-access-token",{issuer});
+    expect(() => authorizeJaspicAdminOffline(user,{
+      expectedSubject: id, expectedEmail: email, expectedIssuer: issuer,
+    })).toThrow("JASPIC admin access denied");
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..45fad612
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,60 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { createSupabaseOAuthOnceStore, type JaspicOAuthDbClient } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-supabase-store";
+
+const challenge = {
+  stateHash: "a".repeat(64),
+  ownerId: "valid_owner_1234",
+  expiresAt: 1_800_000_300_000,
+};
+
+function fakeDb() {
+  const calls: string[] = [];
+  const values = new Map<string, { owner: string; expires: number }>();
+  const db: JaspicOAuthDbClient = {
+    from(table) {
+      expect(table).toBe("jaspic_gmail_oauth_states");
+      return {
+        async insert(row) {
+          calls.push("insert");
+          values.set(row.state_hash, { owner: row.owner_id, expires: Date.parse(row.expires_at) });
+          return { data: null, error: null };
+        },
+      };
+    },
+    async rpc(fn, params) {
+      expect(fn).toBe("jaspic_consume_gmail_oauth_state");
+      calls.push("rpc");
+      const row = values.get(params.p_state_hash);
+      if (!row || row.owner !== params.p_owner_id) return { data: false, error: null };
+      values.delete(params.p_state_hash);
+      return { data: true, error: null };
+    },
+  };
+  return { calls, db };
+}
+
+describe("JASPIC Supabase OAuth state adapter (mock database only)", () => {
+  it("inserts hash-only state and consumes through a single atomic RPC", async () => {
+    const { calls, db } = fakeDb();
+    const store = createSupabaseOAuthOnceStore(db);
+    await store.create(challenge);
+    expect(await store.consumeAtomically({
+      stateHash: challenge.stateHash, ownerId: challenge.ownerId, now: challenge.expiresAt - 1000,
+    })).toBe("consumed");
+    expect(await store.consumeAtomically({
+      stateHash: challenge.stateHash, ownerId: challenge.ownerId, now: challenge.expiresAt - 1000,
+    })).toBe("invalid_or_expired");
+    expect(calls).toEqual(["insert", "rpc", "rpc"]);
+  });
+
+  it("fails closed on failed RPC and does not show database error text", async () => {
+    const db: JaspicOAuthDbClient = {
+      from() { return { insert: async () => ({ data: null, error: null }) }; },
+      rpc: async () => ({ data: null, error: { message: "internal" } }),
+    };
+    const store = createSupabaseOAuthOnceStore(db);
+    await expect(store.consumeAtomically({
+      stateHash: challenge.stateHash, ownerId: challenge.ownerId, now: 100,
+    })).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC OAuth state storage unavailable");
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store.test.ts

+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  persistEncryptedRefreshToken, fetchEncryptedRefreshToken,
+  type JaspicVaultDbClient, type VaultRecord,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-vault-store";
+import { encryptGmailRefreshTokenOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-token-vault-offline";
+
+const owner = "example_owner_1234";
+
+describe("encrypted Gmail token vault adapter (mock-only, no DB/network)", () => {
+  it("writes only an encrypted envelope and reads it back", async () => {
+    let row: VaultRecord | null = null;
+    const db: JaspicVaultDbClient = {
+      from() {
+        return {
+          upsert: async (value) => {
+            row = value;
+            return {data:null,error:null};
+          },
+          select: () => ({eq: () => ({is: () => ({
+            maybeSingle: async () => ({data:row,error:null}),
+          })})}),
+        };
+      },
+    };
+    const envelope = await encryptGmailRefreshTokenOffline(
+      "mock-refresh-token-12345", owner, "a".repeat(43), "testkey_2026",
+    );
+    await persistEncryptedRefreshToken(db, owner, envelope);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(row)).not.toContain("mock-refresh-token-12345");
+    expect(await fetchEncryptedRefreshToken(db, owner)).toEqual(envelope);
+  });
+
+  it("fails closed on database errors and rejects raw tokens", async () => {
+    const db: JaspicVaultDbClient = {
+      from() {
+        return {
+          upsert: async () => ({data:null,error:{message:"private-error"}}),
+          select: () => ({eq: () => ({is: () => ({
+            maybeSingle: async () => ({data:null,error:{message:"private-error"}}),
+          })})}),
+        };
+      },
+    };
+    await expect(persistEncryptedRefreshToken(db,owner,{
+      version:1,keyId:"testkey_2026",iv:"x".repeat(16),ciphertext:"y".repeat(32),
+    })).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC token vault unavailable");
+    await expect(fetchEncryptedRefreshToken(db,owner)).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC token vault unavailable");
+  });
+});
```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-offline-pipeline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-offline-pipeline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-offline-pipeline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..d377b792
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-offline-pipeline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,35 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import fixtures from "./mime-fixtures.json";
+import { generateEml } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime";
+import { prepareGmailRawOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-dry-run";
+import { prepareGmailSendRequestOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-api-request-offline";
+import { simulateGmailDelivery, type SimulatedDelivery } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator";
+import type { Kind, RecordData } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail";
+
+const identity = { date: "Wed, 04 Nov 2026 18:00:00 +0900", messageId: "offline-test@localhost.invalid" };
+const decode = (raw: string): string => Buffer.from(raw.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/"), "base64").toString("utf8");
+
+describe("end-to-end Gmail preparation (OFFLINE; no API/SMTP connection)", () => {
+  for (const f of fixtures) {
+    it(`${f.kind}: ${f.previous === null ? "new" : "update"} raw/body preservation`, () => {
+      const old = Buffer.from(generateEml(f.kind as Kind, f.current as RecordData,
+        f.previous as RecordData | null, f, "eight-bit", identity)).toString("utf8");
+      const prepared = prepareGmailRawOffline(new TextEncoder().encode(old));
+      const request = prepareGmailSendRequestOffline(prepared.raw, "fake-token");
+      const newMail = decode(JSON.parse(request.body).raw as string);
+      expect(request.method).toBe("POST");
+      expect(newMail.split("\r\n\r\n").slice(1).join("\r\n\r\n"))
+        .toBe(old.split("\r\n\r\n").slice(1).join("\r\n\r\n"));
+      expect(newMail).toBe(old.replace("From: hppmap@kishibojim.online",
+        "From: miyazaki1016@gmail.com"));
+      expect(newMail).not.toContain("fake-token");
+    });
+  }
+  it("prevents retry after synthetic ambiguous response", () => {
+    const initial: SimulatedDelivery = { key: "shiga-person:synthetic-42", state: "NEW", attempted: false };
+    const result = simulateGmailDelivery(initial, { scenario: "timeout" });
+    expect(result.state).toBe("UNKNOWN");
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery(result, { scenario: "accepted",
+      syntheticMessageId: "synthetic-42" })).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..729792dd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,35 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  simulateGmailDelivery, type SimulatedDelivery,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-transport-simulator";
+
+const fresh = (): SimulatedDelivery => ({ key: "hiroshima-crime:synthetic-id", state: "NEW", attempted: false });
+describe("Gmail delivery simulator - NO external connections", () => {
+  it("accepts a synthetic result once and blocks any repeat", () => {
+    const result = simulateGmailDelivery(fresh(), { scenario: "accepted", syntheticMessageId: "synthetic-123" });
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      key: "hiroshima-crime:synthetic-id", state: "ACCEPTED", attempted: true,
+      syntheticMessageId: "synthetic-123",
+    });
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery(result, { scenario: "accepted", syntheticMessageId: "synthetic-123" })).toThrow();
+  });
+  it("never automatically retries unknown or failed outcomes", () => {
+    const timedOut = simulateGmailDelivery(fresh(), { scenario: "timeout" });
+    expect(timedOut.state).toBe("UNKNOWN");
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery(timedOut, { scenario: "accepted", syntheticMessageId: "synthetic-123" })).toThrow();
+
+    const rejected = simulateGmailDelivery(fresh(), { scenario: "rejected" });
+    expect(rejected.state).toBe("FAILED");
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery(rejected, { scenario: "accepted", syntheticMessageId: "synthetic-123" })).toThrow();
+  });
+  it("rejects invalid keys, fake IDs and pre-attempted records", () => {
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery({ ...fresh(), key: "x\r\nInjected: y" }, { scenario: "timeout" })).toThrow();
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery(fresh(), { scenario: "accepted", syntheticMessageId: "real-looking-id" })).toThrow();
+    expect(() => simulateGmailDelivery({ ...fresh(), attempted: true }, { scenario: "timeout" })).toThrow();
+  });
+  it("does not mutate the stored record", () => {
+    const before = Object.freeze(fresh());
+    simulateGmailDelivery(before, { scenario: "timeout" });
+    expect(before).toEqual({ key: "hiroshima-crime:synthetic-id", state: "NEW", attempted: false });
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..5ab487cb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { verifyGmailWebCallbackOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-callback-offline";
+import { hashGmailOAuthStateOffline, type OAuthOnceStore } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline";
+import { sealGmailWebSessionOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline";
+
+describe("offline owner-gated one-time callback orchestration", () => {
+  const owner = "owner_subject_1234";
+  const email = "owner@example.invalid";
+  const issuer = "https://auth.example.invalid";
+  const state = "s".repeat(43);
+  const verifier = "v".repeat(64);
+  const key = "a".repeat(43);
+  const now = 1_800_000_000_000;
+
+  function store(): OAuthOnceStore {
+    const records = new Map<string, {ownerId: string; expiresAt: number}>();
+    return {
+      async create(input) { if (records.has(input.stateHash)) throw new Error("Duplicate state"); records.set(input.stateHash, input); },
+      async consumeAtomically({stateHash,ownerId,now:clock}) {
+        const row = records.get(stateHash);
+        if (!row || row.ownerId !== ownerId || row.expiresAt <= clock) return "invalid_or_expired";
+        records.delete(stateHash);
+        return "consumed";
+      },
+    };
+  }
+
+  async function fixture() {
+    const db = store();
+    await db.create({stateHash: await hashGmailOAuthStateOffline(state), ownerId: owner, expiresAt: now + 300000});
+    const encryptedCookie = await sealGmailWebSessionOffline(state, verifier, key, now);
+    return {
+      callbackUrl: "https://emergencyalert.news/jaspic/oauth/callback?code=synthetic&state=" + state,
+      encryptedCookie, cookieEncryptionKey: key,
+      identity: {subject: owner, verifiedEmail: email, issuer, sessionVerified: true as const},
+      adminPolicy: {expectedSubject: owner, expectedEmail: email, expectedIssuer: issuer},
+      store: db, now: now + 1000,
+    };
+  }
+
+  it("returns server-only code and verifier once, then denies replay", async () => {
+    const f = await fixture();
+    expect(await verifyGmailWebCallbackOffline(f)).toEqual({
+      authorizationCode: "synthetic", codeVerifier: verifier, ownerId: owner,
+    });
+    await expect(verifyGmailWebCallbackOffline(f)).rejects.toThrow("OAuth callback rejected");
+  });
+
+  it("denies mismatched owner and mismatched callback CSRF", async () => {
+    const f = await fixture();
+    await expect(verifyGmailWebCallbackOffline({...f, identity: {
+      ...f.identity, subject: "wrong_subject_1234",
+    }})).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC admin access denied");
+    await expect(verifyGmailWebCallbackOffline({...f,
+      callbackUrl: f.callbackUrl.replace(state, "x".repeat(43)),
+    })).rejects.toThrow("Invalid OAuth session");
+  });
+});

```

```text
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-exchange-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { prepareWebOAuthExchangeOffline, validateWebOAuthTokenOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-exchange-offline";
+
+describe("offline OAuth exchange preparation", () => {
+  it("builds a form-encoded request descriptor without a network call", () => {
+    const r = prepareWebOAuthExchangeOffline({
+      clientId: "example.apps.googleusercontent.com",
+      clientSecret: "example",
+      code: "example",
+      codeVerifier: "x".repeat(64),
+    });
+    expect(r.method).toBe("POST");
+    const fields = new URLSearchParams(r.body);
+    expect(fields.get("grant_type")).toBe("authorization_code");
+    expect(fields.get("redirect_uri")).toBe("https://emergencyalert.news/jaspic/oauth/callback");
+  });
+  it("rejects malformed input and incomplete responses", () => {
+    expect(() => prepareWebOAuthExchangeOffline({
+      clientId: "invalid", clientSecret: "example", code: "example", codeVerifier: "x".repeat(64),
+    })).toThrow();
+    expect(() => validateWebOAuthTokenOffline({})).toThrow();
+    const token = {
+      [REDACTED], [REDACTED],
+      expires_in: 3600, token_type: "Bearer",
+      scope: "openid email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send",
+    };
+    expect(validateWebOAuthTokenOffline(token).refreshToken).toBe("synthetic-refresh");
+    expect(() => validateWebOAuthTokenOffline({ ...token, scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send" })).toThrow();
+    expect(() => validateWebOAuthTokenOffline({ ...token, scope: "openid email" })).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-web-http-locked.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-http-locked.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-http-locked.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..a6e3efc3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-http-locked.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { GET as start, POST as startPost } from "../../app/jaspic/oauth/start/route";
+import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
+import { GET as callback } from "../../app/jaspic/oauth/callback/route";
+
+describe("JASPIC iPhone OAuth HTTP endpoints remain locked", () => {
+  it("does not redirect to Google before real server-side owner auth exists", async () => {
+    const response = await start();
+    expect(response.status).toBe(503);
+    expect(response.headers.get("location")).toBeNull();
+    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toContain("no-store");
+    expect(JSON.stringify(await response.json())).not.toContain("client_secret");
+  });
+
+  it("denies POST by default without contacting Google or Supabase", async () => {
+    const response = await startPost(new NextRequest("https://emergencyalert.news/jaspic/oauth/start", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { origin: "https://emergencyalert.news", authorization: "[REDACTED]" },
+    }));
+    expect(response.status).toBe(503);
+    expect(response.headers.get("location")).toBeNull();
+    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toContain("no-store");
+  });
+
+  it("does not echo a returned authorization code or callback state", async () => {
+    const response = await callback(new NextRequest("https://emergencyalert.news/jaspic/oauth/callback?code=synthetic-secret-value&state=" + "s".repeat(43)));
+    expect(response.status).toBe(303);
+    expect(response.headers.get("location")).toBe("https://emergencyalert.news/jaspic/oauth/result?status=unavailable");
+    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toContain("no-store");
+    expect(response.headers.get("referrer-policy")).toBe("no-referrer");
+    const body = await response.text();
+    expect(body).not.toContain("code=");
+    expect(body).not.toContain("state=");
+    expect(body).not.toContain("access_token");
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..b62de23b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { prepareGmailWebConsentOffline, validateGmailWebCallbackOffline, JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-oauth-offline";
+
+describe("iPhone-compatible Gmail web OAuth PKCE, offline only", () => {
+  const clientId = "synthetic-web-client.apps.googleusercontent.com";
+  it("generates single-account consent URL, S256 PKCE and distinct CSRF state", async () => {
+    const first = await prepareGmailWebConsentOffline(clientId);
+    const next = await prepareGmailWebConsentOffline(clientId);
+    const u = new URL(first.authorizationUrl);
+    expect(u.origin).toBe("https://accounts.google.com");
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("client_id")).toBe(clientId);
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("redirect_uri")).toBe(JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK);
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("scope")).toBe("openid email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send");
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("code_challenge_method")).toBe("S256");
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("code_challenge")).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("access_type")).toBe("offline");
+    expect(u.searchParams.get("prompt")).toBe("consent");
+    expect(first.codeVerifier).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{64}$/);
+    expect(first.state).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
+    expect(next.state).not.toBe(first.state);
+    expect(next.codeVerifier).not.toBe(first.codeVerifier);
+    expect(first.authorizationUrl).not.toContain(first.codeVerifier);
+  });
+  it("validates the exact callback and rejects spoofing / multiple state parameters", async () => {
+    const {state} = await prepareGmailWebConsentOffline(clientId);
+    expect(validateGmailWebCallbackOffline(`${JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK}?state=${state}&code=4%2F0Synthetic`, state))
+      .toEqual({ code: "4/0Synthetic" });
+    for (const url of [
+      `https://evil.example/jaspic/oauth/callback?state=${state}&code=4%2F0Synthetic`,
+      `${JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK}?state=${state}&state=${state}&code=4%2F0Synthetic`,
+      `${JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK}?state=wrong&code=4%2F0Synthetic`,
+      `${JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK}?state=${state}&error=access_denied&code=4%2F0Synthetic`,
+      `${JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK}?state=${state}&code=4%2F0Synthetic#fragment`,
+      `${JASPIC_GOOGLE_CALLBACK}?state=${state}&code=x%0AInjected`,
+    ]) expect(() => validateGmailWebCallbackOffline(url,state)).toThrow();
+  });
+  it("rejects malformed or desktop OAuth IDs", async () => {
+    await expect(prepareGmailWebConsentOffline("not-an-oauth-client")).rejects.toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..5c11089d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY,
+  openGmailWebSessionOffline,
+  sealGmailWebSessionOffline,
+} from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline";
+
+describe("offline iPhone OAuth cookie session", () => {
+  const key = "a".repeat(43);
+  const state = "b".repeat(43);
+  const verifier = "c".repeat(64);
+  const now = 1_800_000_000_000;
+
+  it("round trips encrypted state/verifier without putting them in plaintext cookies", async () => {
+    const cookie = await sealGmailWebSessionOffline(state, verifier, key, now);
+    expect(cookie).toMatch(/^v1\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/);
+    expect(cookie).not.toContain(state);
+    expect(cookie).not.toContain(verifier);
+    const result = await openGmailWebSessionOffline(cookie, state, key, now + 10_000);
+    expect(result).toEqual({ state, codeVerifier: verifier, issuedAt: now, expiresAt: now + 300_000 });
+  });
+
+  it("rejects a changed state, tampering, expiry and a wrong key", async () => {
+    const cookie = await sealGmailWebSessionOffline(state, verifier, key, now);
+    await expect(openGmailWebSessionOffline(cookie, "d".repeat(43), key, now + 20)).rejects.toThrow("Invalid OAuth session");
+    await expect(openGmailWebSessionOffline(cookie.slice(0, cookie.lastIndexOf(".") + 1) + (cookie[cookie.lastIndexOf(".") + 1] === "A" ? "B" : "A") + cookie.slice(cookie.lastIndexOf(".") + 2), state, key, now + 20)).rejects.toThrow("Invalid OAuth session");
+    await expect(openGmailWebSessionOffline(cookie, state, key, now + 300_001)).rejects.toThrow("Invalid OAuth session");
+    await expect(openGmailWebSessionOffline(cookie, state, "e".repeat(43), now + 20)).rejects.toThrow("Invalid OAuth session");
+  });
+
+  it("requires a secure HttpOnly host-only cookie policy", () => {
+    expect(GMAIL_OAUTH_COOKIE_POLICY).toMatchObject({
+      name: "__Host-jaspic-oauth", path: "/", httpOnly: true,
+      secure: true, sameSite: "lax", maxAge: 300,
+    });
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..81fafc48
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { prepareAdminGmailOAuthStart } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-start-offline";
+import { openGmailWebSessionOffline } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-web-session-offline";
+import { hashGmailOAuthStateOffline, type OAuthOnceStore } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/gmail-oauth-once-offline";
+
+const owner = "owner_subject_1234";
+const identity = {subject:owner,verifiedEmail:"owner@example.invalid",issuer:"https://auth.example.invalid",sessionVerified:true as const};
+const policy = {expectedSubject:owner,expectedEmail:identity.verifiedEmail,expectedIssuer:identity.issuer};
+const key = "a".repeat(43);
+const now = 1_800_000_000_000;
+
+function testStore() {
+  const registrations: Array<{stateHash:string;ownerId:string;expiresAt:number}> = [];
+  const store: OAuthOnceStore = {
+    create: async (v) => { registrations.push(v); },
+    consumeAtomically: async () => "invalid_or_expired",
+  };
+  return {store,registrations};
+}
+describe("admin-only OAuth start orchestration (offline)", () => {
+  it("registers the opaque state before providing a Google URL and sealed cookie", async () => {
+    const {store,registrations} = testStore();
+    const result = await prepareAdminGmailOAuthStart({
+      identity, adminPolicy:policy, clientId:"test.apps.googleusercontent.com",
+      sessionEncryptionKey:key,stateStore:store,now,
+    });
+    const consent = new URL(result.authorizationUrl);
+    expect(consent.origin).toBe("https://accounts.google.com");
+    expect(result.cookiePolicy).toMatchObject({httpOnly:true,secure:true,sameSite:"lax",maxAge:300});
+    const state = consent.searchParams.get("state")!;
+    expect(registrations).toEqual([{
+      stateHash:await hashGmailOAuthStateOffline(state),ownerId:owner,expiresAt:now+300_000,
+    }]);
+    const session = await openGmailWebSessionOffline(result.sealedCookie,state,key,now+1000);
+    expect(session.state).toBe(state);
+    expect(consent.searchParams.get("code_challenge")).not.toBe(session.codeVerifier);
+    expect(result.authorizationUrl).not.toContain(session.codeVerifier);
+  });
+  it("does not attempt state registration for an unrecognized admin", async () => {
+    const {store,registrations} = testStore();
+    await expect(prepareAdminGmailOAuthStart({
+      identity:{...identity,subject:"another_subject_1234"},adminPolicy:policy,
+      clientId:"test.apps.googleusercontent.com",sessionEncryptionKey:key,stateStore:store,now,
+    })).rejects.toThrow("JASPIC admin access denied");
+    expect(registrations).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+  it("fails closed if the persistent state backend fails", async () => {
+    const store: OAuthOnceStore = {
+      create: async () => {throw new Error("backend unavailable");},
+      consumeAtomically: async () => "invalid_or_expired",
+    };
+    await expect(prepareAdminGmailOAuthStart({
+      identity,adminPolicy:policy,clientId:"test.apps.googleusercontent.com",
+      sessionEncryptionKey:key,stateStore:store,now,
+    })).rejects.toThrow("backend unavailable");
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/handler.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/handler.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/handler.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..6bca47ca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/handler.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+import { expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import fixtures from "./fixtures.json";
+import { collect } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect";
+import { createHandler } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/handler";
+import { plan } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state";
+const f = fixtures[0];
+const item = {kind:"hiroshima-crime" as const,data:f.current,context:f};
+const req = (body: unknown, token = "local-test") => new Request("http://localhost",{method:"POST",headers:{"x-jaspic-token":token},body:JSON.stringify(body)});
+it("hard rejects send mode, unauthenticated requests and unapproved persistence", async () => {
+  const load = vi.fn(); const commit = vi.fn();
+  const handler = createHandler("hiroshima",{token:"local-test",persistAllowed:false,store:{load,commit,legacyIds:async()=>new Set<string>()}});
+  expect((await handler(req({mode:"send"}))).status).toBe(400);
+  expect((await handler(req({mode:"dry-run"},"wrong"))).status).toBe(401);
+  expect((await handler(req({mode:"dry-run",persist:true}))).status).toBe(403);
+  expect(load).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+});
+it("two provinces run independently; failure cannot commit or leak source data", async () => {
+  const load = vi.fn(async () => ({revision:0,baseline:false,records:{}}));
+  const commit = vi.fn(); const store={load,commit,legacyIds:async()=>new Set<string>()};
+  const h = createHandler("hiroshima",{token:"local-test",persistAllowed:true,store,collect:async()=>[item]});
+  const s = createHandler("shiga",{token:"local-test",persistAllowed:true,store,collect:async()=>{throw new Error("private source payload");}});
+  const [a,b] = await Promise.all([h(req({mode:"dry-run",persist:true})),s(req({mode:"dry-run",persist:true}))]);
+  expect(await a.json()).toMatchObject({baseline:true,candidates:0,sent:0,persisted:true});
+  expect(b.status).toBe(503); expect(await b.text()).not.toContain("private");
+  expect(commit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); expect(commit.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe("hiroshima");
+});
+it("dry-run preview never writes, and large count drops leave previous baseline intact", async () => {
+  const p = await plan("hiroshima",{revision:0,baseline:false,records:{}},[item]);
+  const records = Object.fromEntries(Array.from({length:10},(_,i)=>[`hiroshima-crime:${i}`,item.data]));
+  const commit=vi.fn();
+  const handler=createHandler("hiroshima",{token:"local-test",persistAllowed:true,store:{load:async()=>({revision:1,baseline:true,records}),commit,legacyIds:async()=>new Set<string>()},collect:async()=>[item]});
+  expect((await handler(req({mode:"dry-run",persist:true}))).status).toBe(503);
+  expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  const preview=createHandler("hiroshima",{token:"local-test",persistAllowed:true,store:{load:async()=>({...p,revision:1}),commit,legacyIds:async()=>new Set<string>()},collect:async()=>[item]});
+  expect((await preview(req({mode:"dry-run"}))).status).toBe(200);
+  expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+});
+it("collection rejects HTTP errors, incomplete/empty/duplicate/schema-changed source", async () => {
+  await expect(collect("shiga",async()=>new Response("not found",{status:404}))).rejects.toThrow("404");
+  const geo={features:[{properties:{city_code:f.current.city_code,full_name:"架空市"}}]};
+  const source=fixtures.find(f=>f.kind==="shiga-person")!.current;
+  for (const data of [[],[source,source],[{...source, id:null}],[{id:"missing-fields"}],[{...source,latitude:34.0000}]]) {
+    await expect(collect("shiga",async(url)=>Response.json(String(url).includes("geo")?geo:data))).rejects.toThrow();
+  }
+});
+it("Shiga direct JSON is supported without a browser and keeps original field strings", async () => {
+  const f=fixtures.find(f=>f.kind==="shiga-person")!;
+  const mock=vi.fn(async(url: RequestInfo | URL)=>Response.json(String(url).includes("geo")?{features:[{properties:{city_code:f.current.city_code,full_name:"架空市"}}]}:[f.current]));
+  const items=await collect("shiga",mock);
+  expect(items).toHaveLength(1); expect(items[0].data.latitude).toBe("34.0000");
+  expect(mock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
+});
+
+it("fails closed when legacy Hiroshima ID history cannot be read, without committing or leaking details", async () => {
+  const commit = vi.fn();
+  const legacyIds = vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error("private database error"); });
+  const handler = createHandler("hiroshima", {
+    token: "local-test", persistAllowed: true,
+    store: {
+      load: async () => ({ revision: 0, baseline: false, records: {} }),
+      commit, legacyIds,
+    },
+    collect: async () => [item],
+  });
+  const response = await handler(req({ mode: "dry-run", persist: true }));
+  expect(response.status).toBe(503);
+  const text = await response.text();
+  expect(text).not.toContain("private");
+  expect(JSON.parse(text)).toMatchObject({ sent: 0, error: "JASPIC_RUN_FAILED" });
+  expect(legacyIds).toHaveBeenCalledWith("hiroshima");
+  expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/mail.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/mail.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/mail.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..e76d2392
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/mail.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import fixtures from "./fixtures.json";
+import expected from "./expected.json";
+import { generateMail, month, type Kind, type RecordData } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail";
+import { renderShigaText } from "../../lib/jaspic/shigaMail";
+
+describe("legacy C# body byte parity", () => {
+  fixtures.forEach((f, index) => it(`${f.kind} fixture ${index}`, () => {
+    const mail = generateMail(f.kind as Kind, f.current as RecordData, f.previous as RecordData | null, f);
+    expect(Buffer.from(mail.body, "utf8")).toEqual(Buffer.from(expected[index].body, "utf8"));
+    expect(mail.subject).toBe(` [JASPIC-NES] ${f.kind === "shiga-person" ? "滋賀" : "広島"}県警専用ツール`);
+    expect(mail.from).toBe("hppmap@kishibojim.online");
+    expect(mail.to).toBe("getter01@jaspic-nes.net");
+    expect(mail.cc).toBe(f.kind === "shiga-person" ? "kishibojim@gmail.com" : "miyazaki1016@me.com");
+    expect(mail.body.replaceAll("\r\n", "")).not.toMatch(/[\r\n]/);
+    expect(mail.body.endsWith("\r\n")).toBe(true);
+    if (f.kind === "shiga-person") expect(renderShigaText(f.current, f.previous, f.cityName)).toBe(mail.body);
+  }));
+  it("rejects invalid dates and categories rather than silently changing output", () => {
+    expect(() => month("2026/02/30")).toThrow();
+    const f = fixtures.find(f => f.kind === "shiga-person")!;
+    expect(() => generateMail("shiga-person", { ...f.current, case_type: "10" } as RecordData, null, f)).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/mime.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/mime.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/mime.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3bb57469
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/mime.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import fixtures from "./mime-fixtures.json";
+import { generateEml, type MimeMode } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime";
+import { generateMail, type Kind, type RecordData } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail";
+// @ts-expect-error Node-only comparator is JavaScript.
+import { compareEml } from "../../scripts/compare-jaspic-eml.mjs";
+const identity={date:"Wed, 04 Nov 2026 18:00:00 +0900",messageId:"synthetic-jaspic@localhost.invalid"};
+describe("offline MimeKit 4.12 parity",()=>{
+  for(const f of fixtures) for(const mode of ["unprepared","seven-bit","eight-bit"] as MimeMode[]) {
+    const name=f.kind+(f.previous===null?"-new":"-update")+"-"+mode+".eml";
+    it(name,()=>{
+      const old=readFileSync(join(process.env.JASPIC_MIME_ORACLE_DIR??"tests/jaspic/mime-golden",name));
+      const next=Buffer.from(generateEml(f.kind as Kind,f.current as RecordData,f.previous as RecordData|null,f,mode,identity));
+      expect(next).toEqual(old); // full bytes, even Date/Message-ID (no exclusions)
+      expect(compareEml(old,next,{stage:mode==="unprepared"?"unprepared":"prepared"})).toBe(true);
+      const raw=next.toString("utf8");
+      const [header,...body]=raw.split("\r\n\r\n");
+      expect(header).toContain("charset=utf-8");
+      expect(header).toContain("Subject: [JASPIC-NES] =?utf-8?b?");
+      expect(generateMail(f.kind as Kind,f.current as RecordData,f.previous as RecordData|null,f).subject.startsWith(" ")).toBe(true);
+      const wireBody=body.join("\r\n\r\n");
+      const decoded=mode==="seven-bit"?Buffer.from(wireBody,"base64").toString("utf8"):wireBody;
+      expect(decoded).toBe(generateMail(f.kind as Kind,f.current as RecordData,f.previous as RecordData|null,f).body);
+      expect(raw.replaceAll("\r\n","")).not.toMatch(/[\r\n]/);
+    });
+  }
+  it("rejects unverified QP/long lines and MIME identity injection",()=>{
+    const f=fixtures[0];
+    for(const address of ["x".repeat(1000),"x\r\nFrom synthetic-marker","x\r\n".repeat(2000)]) {
+      expect(()=>generateEml(f.kind as Kind,{...f.current,address} as RecordData,null,f,"seven-bit",identity)).toThrow();
+    }
+    expect(()=>generateEml(f.kind as Kind,f.current as RecordData,null,f,"eight-bit",{...identity,date:"x\r\nInjected: y"})).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/real-mail.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/real-mail.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/real-mail.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..e5592c49
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/real-mail.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,45 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import fixtures from './fixtures.json';
+import { generateMail, type Kind, type RecordData } from '../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail';
+import { generateEml } from '../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mime';
+import { verifyRealMail } from '../../scripts/jaspic-verify-real-mail';
+
+const fixture = fixtures.find(f => f.kind === 'shiga-person' && !f.previous)!;
+const item = { kind: fixture.kind as Kind, data: fixture.current as unknown as RecordData, context: fixture };
+const mail = generateMail(item.kind, item.data, null, item.context);
+const raw = (body = mail.body, subject = mail.subject.slice(1), cte = '8bit') => Buffer.from(
+  `Subject: =?utf-8?b?${Buffer.from(subject).toString('base64')}?=\r\nContent-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8\r\nContent-Transfer-Encoding: ${cte}\r\n\r\n${body}`, 'utf8');
+
+describe('private raw mail verification', () => {
+  it('compares raw UTF-8 bytes, preserving the logical/serialized subject distinction', () => {
+    expect(verifyRealMail(raw(), [item])).toMatchObject({ bodyBytesMatch: true, crlfOnly: true, subjectLogicalMatch: false, subjectSerializedValueMatch: true });
+  });
+  it('detects changed values, fullwidth spaces, URLs, and missing final CRLF', () => {
+    for (const body of [mail.body.replace('　', ' '), mail.body.replace('google.com', 'example.invalid'), mail.body.slice(0, -2), mail.body.replace('→管理番号', '→番号')]) {
+      expect(verifyRealMail(raw(body), [item]).bodyBytesMatch).toBe(false);
+    }
+  });
+  it('does not normalize LF into a false byte match', () => {
+    expect(() => verifyRealMail(raw(mail.body.replaceAll('\r\n', '\n')), [item])).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('rejects unsupported encodings, invalid UTF-8, missing and duplicate source IDs', () => {
+    expect(() => verifyRealMail(raw(mail.body, undefined, 'base64'), [item])).toThrow();
+    expect(() => verifyRealMail(Buffer.concat([raw(), Buffer.from([255])]), [item])).toThrow();
+    expect(() => verifyRealMail(raw(), [])).toThrow();
+    expect(() => verifyRealMail(raw(), [item, item])).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('keeps update projection separate from unavailable previous-value verification', () => {
+    const old = { ...item.data, address: '合成更新前' };
+    const updated = generateMail(item.kind, item.data, old, item.context);
+    expect(verifyRealMail(raw(updated.body), [item])).toMatchObject({ update: true, bodyBytesMatch: true, currentValuesAndStructureMatch: true, observedUpdateMarkers: 1, previousValuesVerified: false });
+    expect(() => verifyRealMail(raw(updated.body.replace('→発生場所●更新', '●更新→発生場所')), [item])).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('checks encoded Subject bytes separately from decoded text', () => {
+    const eml = Buffer.from(generateEml(item.kind, item.data, null, item.context, 'eight-bit', { date: 'Wed, 04 Nov 2026 00:00:00 +0000', messageId: 'synthetic.invalid' }));
+    expect(verifyRealMail(eml, [item]).subjectHeaderBytesMatch).toBe(true);
+    expect(verifyRealMail(raw(), [item])).toMatchObject({ subjectSerializedValueMatch: true, subjectHeaderBytesMatch: false });
+  });
+  it('detects a different subject', () => {
+    expect(verifyRealMail(raw(mail.body, '別件名'), [item]).subjectSerializedValueMatch).toBe(false);
+  });
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/state.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/state.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/state.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..169b5e08
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/state.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,60 @@
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import fixtures from "./fixtures.json";
+import { plan, transition, type Snapshot } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/state";
+import type { Item } from "../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/collect";
+const fixture = fixtures[0];
+const item: Item = { kind: "hiroshima-crime", data: fixture.current, context: fixture };
+const empty: Snapshot = { revision: 0, baseline: false, records: {} };
+it("baseline is silent; identical/coordinate-only runs are silent; update is one candidate", async () => {
+  const initial = await plan("hiroshima", empty, [item]);
+  expect(initial.candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+  const snapshot = { ...initial, revision: 1 };
+  expect((await plan("hiroshima", snapshot, [item])).candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+  const coord = { ...item, data: { ...item.data, latitude: "35" } };
+  const unchanged = await plan("hiroshima", snapshot, [coord]);
+  expect(unchanged.candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+  expect(unchanged.records["hiroshima-crime:TEST-0001"].latitude).toBe(item.data.latitude);
+  const changed = { ...coord, data: { ...coord.data, address: "架空更新" } };
+  const result = await plan("hiroshima", snapshot, [changed]);
+  expect(result.candidates).toHaveLength(1);
+  expect(result.candidates[0].mail.body).toContain('"latitude":"35",　→緯度（参考程度）●更新');
+  expect((await plan("hiroshima", { ...result, revision: 2 }, [changed])).candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+});
+it("keeps old IDs after disappearance and never promotes an empty/duplicate/mixed baseline", async () => {
+  const initial = await plan("hiroshima", empty, [item]);
+  const second = { ...item, data: { ...item.data, id: "OTHER" } };
+  const next = await plan("hiroshima", { ...initial, revision: 1 }, [second]);
+  expect(next.records["hiroshima-crime:TEST-0001"]).toBeDefined();
+  expect((await plan("hiroshima", { ...next, revision: 2 }, [item])).candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+  await expect(plan("hiroshima", empty, [])).rejects.toThrow();
+  await expect(plan("hiroshima", empty, [item,item])).rejects.toThrow();
+  await expect(plan("shiga", empty, [item])).rejects.toThrow();
+});
+it("preserves exclusion, legacy similar-ID check and historical date cutoff", async () => {
+  const baseline = await plan("hiroshima", empty, [item]);
+  const snapshot = { ...baseline, revision: 1 };
+  for (const data of [{...item.data,id:"NEW",case_type:"自転車盗"},{...item.data,id:"NEW",occurrence_date_from:"2023/05/31 23:59:00"},{...item.data,id:"TEST-0001-more"}]) {
+    expect((await plan("hiroshima", snapshot, [{...item,data}])).candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+  }
+});
+it("never retries UNKNOWN, SENT or a claimed delivery", () => {
+  expect(transition(transition("PREPARED","claim"),"uncertain")).toBe("UNKNOWN");
+  for (const status of ["SENDING","SENT","UNKNOWN","CANCELLED"] as const) expect(() => transition(status,"claim")).toThrow();
+});
+it("legacy Hiroshima IDs suppress both new and updated person records, without suppressing crime", async () => {
+  const f=fixtures.find(f=>f.kind==="hiroshima-person")!;
+  const person: Item={kind:"hiroshima-person",data:f.current,context:f};
+  const baseline=await plan("hiroshima",empty,[person,item]);
+  const snapshot={...baseline,revision:1};
+  const changed={...person,data:{...person.data,address:"架空更新"}};
+  expect((await plan("hiroshima",snapshot,[changed,item])).candidates).toHaveLength(1);
+  const options={legacyHiroshimaPersonIds:new Set([person.data.id!])};
+  const excluded=await plan("hiroshima",snapshot,[changed,item],options);
+  expect(excluded.candidates).toHaveLength(0);
+  expect(excluded.records["hiroshima-person:"+person.data.id].address).toBe("架空更新");
+  const newPerson={...person,data:{...person.data,id:"LEGACY-REAPPEARED"}};
+  const result=await plan("hiroshima",snapshot,[newPerson,{...item,data:{...item.data,address:"架空犯罪更新"}}],
+    {legacyHiroshimaPersonIds:new Set(["LEGACY-REAPPEARED",item.data.id!])});
+  expect(result.candidates).toHaveLength(1);
+  expect(result.candidates[0].key).toMatch(/^hiroshima-crime:/);
+});

```

### tests/jaspic/update-observation.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/jaspic/update-observation.test.ts b/tests/jaspic/update-observation.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..faacd2b7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/jaspic/update-observation.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import fixtures from './fixtures.json';
+import { generateMail, type Kind, type RecordData } from '../../supabase/functions/_shared/jaspic/mail';
+import { observedItem, markedPrevious, observedFields } from '../../scripts/jaspic-update-observation';
+
+describe('observed update marker reconstruction (A only)', () => {
+  for (const [index, f] of fixtures.entries()) {
+    it(`preserves fixture ${index} body bytes using synthetic previous values`, () => {
+      const kind = f.kind as Kind;
+      const current = f.current as unknown as RecordData;
+      const previous = f.previous as unknown as RecordData | null;
+      const original = generateMail(kind, current, previous, f);
+      const rebuilt = generateMail(kind, current, previous === null ? null : markedPrevious(original.body, kind, current), f);
+      expect(Buffer.from(rebuilt.body)).toEqual(Buffer.from(original.body));
+      if (previous !== null) {
+        for (const [key, value] of observedFields(original.body)) {
+          expect(rebuilt.body.includes('SYNTHETIC_PREVIOUS_VALUE')).toBe(false);
+          expect(markedPrevious(original.body, kind, current)[key] !== current[key]).toBe(value.marked);
+        }
+      }
+    });
+  }
+  it('reconstructs historical displayed values and lookup annotations without a current source', () => {
+    const f = fixtures.find(f => f.kind === 'hiroshima-person' && f.previous)!;
+    const original = generateMail('hiroshima-person', f.current as unknown as RecordData, f.previous as unknown as RecordData, f);
+    const item = observedItem(original.body);
+    const result = generateMail(item.kind, item.data, markedPrevious(original.body, item.kind, item.data), item.context);
+    expect(result.body).toBe(original.body);
+  });
+  it('rejects missing/duplicate fields and unrecognized marker positions', () => {
+    const f = fixtures[0];
+    const body = generateMail(f.kind as Kind, f.current as unknown as RecordData, null, f).body;
+    expect(() => observedItem(body.replace(/^"id":.*\r\n/m, ''))).toThrow();
+    expect(() => observedItem(body.replace('{\r\n', '{\r\n"id":"duplicate"\r\n'))).toThrow();
+    expect(() => observedItem(body.replace('→管理番号', '●更新→管理番号'))).toThrow();
+  });
+});

```

```text
+  });
+  it("fails closed on invalid category", () => {

+  });
+});
```

### 関連資料 docs/DECISIONS.md

```text
# EmergencyAlert Decision Log

## 2026-09 — source of truth

EmergencyAlert does not independently forecast weather. Official/authorized
forecast, observation and disaster information is the source of truth.

## Rain notification v1

The initial lifestyle notification candidate is forecast rain intensity class
5 mm/h or greater within 30 minutes.

This is NOT a meteorological danger threshold. It is a configurable product
threshold intended to answer whether a person may want to act now (for example,
bringing laundry inside).

Rain below 5 mm/h may be shown in the app but does not normally trigger a push.

## Ending rain v1

Do not infer an ending from one zero frame. Require three consecutive valid
NO_RAIN frames (15 minutes at the normal 5-minute cadence). A missing,
unavailable or unknown frame breaks the run.

## Missing data

Never map NO_DATA, OUT_OF_COVERAGE, FETCH_ERROR or UNKNOWN_PIXEL to NO_RAIN.
Never interpolate a missing official frame.

## JMA PNG palette

The historical 2023 RGB palette is not treated as authoritative for 2026.
The current JMA documentation confirms the precipitation intensity product and
its thresholds, but the application must not silently assume an unverified
rendering palette. Until the palette is verified for the current tile product,
opaque unrecognised pixels remain UNKNOWN_PIXEL.

Future Sora: do not "fix" this by copying the 2023 colors without verification.


## Voice and urgency — product constitution

EmergencyAlert speaks in two clearly separated modes.

### Everyday / lifestyle information

Use ordinary, family-like language. Do not sound bureaucratic or technical.
The user should understand the situation at a glance.

Principle:

> かしこまらない。難しくしない。見た瞬間わかる。

Examples of the intended tone:

- 「もうすぐ雨くるよ」
- 「洗濯物いれとこ」
- 「もうすぐやみそう」

Technical evidence, thresholds and source details remain available underneath,
but the main UI translates them into everyday action language.

### Life-safety information

When an official source indicates an urgent threat to life or physical safety,
the voice changes deliberately: short, strong, direct and action-oriented.
Do not soften an urgent official warning into the casual lifestyle voice.

Principle:

> 普段は、家族が声をかけるように。
> 命に関わるときは、ためらわず強く。

Examples include official emergency earthquake warnings, extreme heat alerts,
special warnings, evacuation information, and other authoritative emergency
information. Exact wording and escalation rules must be grounded in the
official source and its defined severity; EmergencyAlert must not invent or
independently upgrade a danger level.

### Voice escalation boundary

The system does not choose an emergency tone merely because its own logic
"feels" that conditions are dangerous. Emergency voice escalation must be
triggered by an authoritative official alert/severity signal that the
integration explicitly understands.

Future Sora: never let a life-safety alert say something merely cute or casual
because the everyday rain UI uses that tone. Conversely, do not make ordinary
lifestyle notices frightening just to attract attention.


## Project continuity — rule zero

This rule applies across the owner's projects, not only EmergencyAlert.

> 第0条：未来のソラを信用するな。
> 第1条：決めたことより「なぜ決めたか」を残せ。
> 第2条：思想は継承する。実装は検証する。

This is not a joke-only convention. Future sessions and future implementations
must be able to recover the product intent without guessing. Record important
decisions together with their rationale, constraints, rejected shortcuts and
safety boundaries. Do not reinterpret a documented product philosophy merely
because a different implementation looks newer or more elegant.


## Trust is accumulated before an emergency

EmergencyAlert must earn trust during ordinary, low-stakes use before asking the
user to act during a life-safety event.

Trust is built by being consistently transparent about:

- when the information was checked;
- which location is being evaluated and, where useful, its location accuracy;
- uncertainty, missing data and unsupported interpretation;
- the difference between "no rain" and "we could not confirm it".

Principle:

> 普段から正直だから、緊急時の強い言葉が届く。

Do not optimize ordinary notifications for fear, urgency or engagement.
Everyday notices should be calm and proportionate. When the system cannot
confirm something, it should say so plainly instead of manufacturing certainty.

The product should therefore treat each ordinary notification as both useful
information and a small deposit of trust. That accumulated trust is what gives
an authoritative emergency message weight when a genuine official life-safety
alert arrives.

Future Sora: never trade this accumulated trust for a more dramatic message,
a prettier success state, or a higher notification rate.


## JMA PNG palette changes — detect automatically, promote manually

Current public-PNG interpretation depends on verified RGB-to-intensity associations, so a JMA presentation change can affect classification even when the precipitation intensity bands themselves remain stable.

Policy:

> 変更は自動で見つける。意味は勝手に決めない。

EmergencyAlert may automatically observe and compare the current JMA legend against the verified production palette. A difference becomes CHANGE_DETECTED and should eventually trigger operational review/alerting.

A detected RGB must **never** be promoted automatically into the production palette. Promotion requires independent evidence that associates the new RGB with the official intensity band. Until then, unverified opaque pixels remain UNKNOWN_PIXEL; they must not become NO_RAIN or another guessed class.

The palette watcher is therefore a detector, not an updater. This preserves the project rule that uncertainty must not be converted into safety.

Long term, an authorized numeric precipitation feed should reduce or remove this presentation-color dependency. The zero-fixed-cost phase continues to use public PNG imagery conservatively.


## Observation freshness is part of truthfulness

A fresh API response must not make stale meteorological evidence look fresh.

For the rain v1 public-imagery path, the interpretation engine currently withholds user-facing claims when the latest observation is more than 15 minutes old (or implausibly more than 5 minutes in the future). JMA high-resolution precipitation nowcast updates on a 5-minute cadence; the 15-minute limit is an operational tolerance, not a meteorological threshold.

This guard is intentionally conservative and may be revised when provider behavior is better characterized. If revised, keep the reason and tests with the change.

> **確認時刻が新しくても、元データが古ければ「最新」として語らない。**


## Do not force symmetry across JMA observation and forecast metadata

Observation and forecast metadata are different products. A validation rule that is valid for forecast frames must not be copied to observation frames merely because the fields have similar names.

In particular, the rain v1 engine validates the shape of both `baseTime` and `validTime`, but it does not impose the forecast-only `baseTime <= validTime` ordering rule on observation frames without provider-specific evidence.

> **同じ名前の項目でも、同じ意味とは決めつけない。**


## Watch locations are explicit saved places, not continuous tracking

EmergencyAlert's server-side monitoring model uses **user-selected watch
targets**. A user intentionally saves a place (for example home, school or a
family member's home), and the server evaluates that saved coordinate on the
monitoring cadence even while the browser/app is closed.

The initial design must **not** continuously track the device's current
location in the background. Current-location lookup remains useful for the
one-shot 「この場所の雨を確認」 flow and may be used to make saving a watch
place easy, but monitoring begins only after the user chooses to watch that
place.

A watch target should eventually persist at least: owner identity, stable target
ID, user-facing label, coordinate, enabled state, notification preferences and
state needed for semantic-event deduplication. Do not store a trail of device
movement merely to implement weather monitoring.

At scale, monitoring must be organized around reusable JMA source work rather
than one upstream fetch per user. Nearby watch targets that depend on the same
JMA tile/frame should share that source fetch, after which EmergencyAlert may
sample/evaluate each saved coordinate independently and notify only targets with
a grounded actionable semantic change.

> 見張る場所はユーザーが決める。ユーザーの移動を見張らない。

```

### 関連資料 docs/JMA_SOURCE_NOTES.md

```text
# JMA source notes

## Confirmed from current JMA documentation

- High-resolution precipitation nowcast is updated every 5 minutes.
- It provides precipitation intensity at 250 m resolution through 30 minutes.
- Forecasts from 35 through 60 minutes are provided at 1 km resolution.
- JMA's information catalogue identifies both analysis and forecast imagery as PNG.
- The public legend uses precipitation-intensity breakpoints 1, 5, 10, 20, 30, 50 and 80 mm/h.

## Not yet promoted to a contract

The internal jmatile path, JSON metadata shape and PNG palette are implementation
details observed on the JMA website. They are isolated inside the JMA provider
so they can be replaced if JMA changes the website.

The numeric legend breakpoints do NOT by themselves prove exact RGB values.
Do not convert an RGB value to a rain class until that palette is verified.

## Product consequence

EmergencyAlert may say what the official JMA series supports. It must not turn
a fetch error, unknown color or missing frame into "no rain" or "safe".


## 2026-09 re-check: official nowcast cadence/resolution

JMA's current help page states that "雨雲の動き" provides:
- through 30 minutes: 5-minute intervals, 250 m grid;
- through 1 hour: 1 km grid.

This supports the 2026 product's use of official successive frames to show
time progression. It does not authorize EmergencyAlert to extrapolate movement
between or beyond JMA frames.

Source: https://www.jma.go.jp/bosai/faq/pc.html

```

```text
# MYアメふる通知フィードバック 第1段階 チェックポイント（2026-10-21）

## ユーザー方針
機能を先に完成。既存本番の通知配信を変えずに、すでに届いた通知に対して「実際はどうだった？」と回答し、保存・変更・再表示できるようにする。通知送信・DB異常対応やクラッシュ/競合対策は **Issue #64** に保存して後日回収。PR #63は第二段階と詳細判定根拠の開発用として維持。

## 第一段階の実装（本番には未反映）
- Draft PR #65 (`feat/rain-feedback-phase1-history-20261021`): 現行`main`から独立。
- `app/components/NotificationFeedbackHistory.tsx`: 最近7日・200件の配信成功履歴、降り始め/止み始め別4択、保存済み、変更、未回答絞り込み。
- `app/page.tsx`: 現行の「今日の最新アラート」からその通知の回答カードへ移動するリンク。**Push payloadは変更しない。**
- `lib/weather/rain/feedbackPhase1.ts` と `tests/feedbackPhase1.test.ts`: URLハッシュの数字ID、否定回答のタイミングのテスト。
- `docs/sql/my-rain-feedback-phase1.sql`: 回答テーブルとユーザー本人だけに読み書きさせるRLS。追加対象は回答テーブルのみで、既存の配信テーブルCHECKやPush workerは変更しない。
- `tests/sql/feedback-phase1-integration.sql` を使い捨てPostgreSQLでCI実行。A/B本人権限、回答作成・変更、他人の通知、サポート外通知への回答拒否を検証。

## 使う側の注意と設計
- 本番に既存の「予測対象時刻」の履歴保存はないため、「降らなかった」「止まなかった」は送信1時間後から回答可能とする**暫定運用**。これを予測的中率の確定測定と混同しない。
- 別地点を現地確認していない場合は「わからない」。現在地と登録地点を混同しない。
- URLハッシュは一覧のスクロールのためだけ。DB読み書き権限は Supabase Auth とRLSで検証する。
- 古い通知をPushタップから直接開く機能は**第二段階**。第一段階は画面内の「今日の最新アラート」から回答へジャンプ。

## 残る本番導入ゲート（現在 NO-GO）
1. GitHub Actionsで最新コミットの `verify` + `phase1-feedback-rls` が成功。
2. 隔離したSupabase Auth + RESTでログイン済みA/Bユーザーに対するRLSと保存・変更・再表示を確認。
3. iPhone Safari/PWAでカード表示、アンカー、保存状態、読み込みエラー、ログインなしを実機確認。
4. 検証後に初めて、対象本番DBのスキーマ再確認、バックアップと復旧手順、**本番DB変更の別途明示承認**、画面公開の別途承認を確認。

## 環境照合
2026-10-21現在、利用可能なSupabaseプロジェクト一覧では、`EmergencyAlert` 本番と**別製品** `misaki` の2つのみ。隔離されたアメふる検証用プロジェクトは確認できていない。美咲にSQL適用しない。本番に試験データを流し込まない。検証環境がなければ実API試験未完と明記する。

## 追補 2026-10-21：本番画面に近い操作動線の改善

- 「今日の最新アラート」の該当行から `#rain-feedback-<id>` で回答カードを開き、強調・スクロール表示。**本番Push payloadは未変更**。
- 同じ画面で見張る場所を登録した際、匿名Authセッション新規発行直後にフィードバック履歴を再読み込みするカスタム通知 `amefuru:auth-ready` を追加。
- Supabaseの認証イベントからも同期コールバック内で直接async APIを実行せず、次のイベントループに処理を回して再取得。Supabase設定がない検証用プレビューではAuth listenerを設置せず、画面自体のクラッシュを防止。
- 回答済みの再確認や通信復帰のため、利用者が押せる「通知履歴を更新」ボタンを追加。
- GitHub CIで `7ba0770d` が一度失敗（ブラウザtimer IDをNodeJS.Timeout扱いした型エラー）。`2a27b3d5` で型を修正して CI success。今回のAuth変更後のHEADでも継続検証。
- 隔離Supabase Auth/API、iPhone PWA実機は引き続き未検証。本番SQL・main・Edge・cron・Pushは未変更。Issue #64は保留のまま。

## 追補：見張り地点と回答対象を混同しないUI（2026-10-21）

- 通知の `target_id` に対応する `watch_targets.id,label` を本人のSupabaseセッションで取得し、通知カードに **「📍 見張っていた場所」** を明示する。取得できなければ地名を推測せず **「登録地点（名称を確認できないよ）」** と表示する。現在地を通知対象として流用しない。
- 複数の見張り地点があるとき、**場所別フィルター**を表示。回答できる件数・未回答件数も選択中の見張り地点について計算する。「今日の最新アラート」からのリンクを開くとフィルターを全件へ戻し、正しい通知が隠れないようにした。
- `lib/weather/rain/feedbackPhase1.ts` の純粋関数と `tests/feedbackPhase1.test.ts` で、地点IDに基づく表示と絞り込み、削除済み地点、別地点へ紛れ込まないことを自動テスト。
- 現時点はPR #65内のステージング向けコード。Push/Edge/main/本番DB未変更。API接続とiPhone実機の確認は未完了、公開NO-GOを維持。

## 追補：回答の保存→再読込→変更の実装と模擬テスト（2026-10-21）

- `lib/weather/rain/feedbackPhase1Repository.ts` に通知回答の保存 `savePhase1Feedback` と再読込 `loadPhase1Feedback` を切り出した。UI `app/components/NotificationFeedbackHistory.tsx` は両方を利用する。
- 保存時はSupabase Authの現在セッションからowner_idを取得し、通知IDを正の安全整数に限定。RLSが本人以外の読書きを拒否する前提を保つ。実際の所有者や送達確認はサーバーRLSの責務。
- 再読込では表示中の配信通知IDだけを指定し、想定外のID/回答値は表示に採用しない。
- `tests/feedbackPhase1Repository.test.ts` に**インメモリの模擬Supabase**による回答作成→再読込→回答変更→再読込、未ログイン時の保存拒否、ID不正、別ユーザーの模擬的な分離を追加した。**模擬テストの成功は実際のSupabase Auth/REST HTTPやiPhone実機の成功証明ではない**。
- 実装・検証が進んでも、本番DBのフィードバックテーブル新設/PRのマージ/本番公開は別の明示承認まで行わない。Issue #64の障害復旧深掘りは再開しない。

## 追補：使い捨てPostgreSQL＋PostgREST の実HTTP試験成功（2026-10-21）

- `tests/sql/feedback-phase1-http-setup.sql`: 本番と無関係なPostgres 16に、配信通知（A/B所有者）・本人限定閲覧RLS・第1段階の回答スキーマを準備する。
- `tests/feedback-phase1-http.mjs`: テスト内で署名した**使い捨てJWT**をHTTPのBearerトークンにして、PostgRESTからA/Bの通知履歴を取得し、回答の新規POST→GET再取得→同じ通知のupsert変更→GET再取得、他人の通知・未対応イベント・不正回答・未認証での保存拒否および他人の回答が見えないことを検証する。
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` の `phase1-feedback-http` jobで実行。PostgREST `v12.2.3`＋PostgreSQL `16`を**CIの一時コンテナのみ**で起動し、終了時に廃棄。Supabase本番にも美咲にも接続しない。
- 最初のHTTP検証時に、模擬 `auth.uid()` がレガシーな `request.jwt.claim.sub` からの取得になっており、実PostgREST v12のJWT claims JSONと不整合を検出。使い捨てDB fixtureで `request.jwt.claims::jsonb ->> 'sub'` を読むよう修正。**製品本番のSQLは変更しない**。
- コミット **`cdbec051`** の GitHub Actions run **`39570989341`** は **`verify`、`phase1-feedback-rls`、`phase1-feedback-http` 全て success**。今回の成果は模擬Supabaseだけでなく、**本物のPostgREST HTTP要求と実Postgres/RLSでの保存・更新・再読込を検証**できた点。
- **注意：これはSupabaseマネージドのAuth/REST接続ではない**。本番とは別のSupabaseプロジェクト/開発ブランチの準備と iPhone実機 PWA 操作検証は引き続き未実施。CI緑 ≠ 本番公開可能。本番DB変更、Edge/Push変更、mainのマージは許可されていない。

## 追補：回答済み通知の見直し（2026-10-21）

- 第1段階のフィードバック履歴に「**すべて／未回答だけ／回答済みだけ**」を追加。対象地点ごとの **回答できる件数・回答済み件数・未回答件数** も表示。
- `lib/weather/rain/feedbackPhase1.ts` に表示件数とフィルター選択を純粋関数化し、`tests/feedbackPhase1.test.ts` で未対応の通知種別の除外、絞り込み、通知リンクで指定した回答済み通知を隠さないことを検証。
- Freeプランで3つ目のSupabaseプロジェクトは作成しない。既存の使い捨てPostgres/PostgREST HTTP試験を維持する。**SupabaseマネージドAuth/RESTおよびiPhone実機の検証は未実施のまま**。
- PR #65はDraft/NO-GO、main/本番DB/Edge/既存Push配信には一切変更なし。運用保留はIssue #64。
```

### 関連資料 docs/MY_RAIN_OBSERVATION_TIME_STAGE_20261022.md

```text
# フィードバックの「実際に天気を見た時刻」第2段階（Issue #70）

## 目的
既存回答の `updated_at`（回答の保存・変更時刻）、通知の `delivered_at`（配信時刻）、実際に現地を見た `observed_at`（観測時刻）を**混同しない**。

## 開発内容（このPRで本番未反映）
- 「確認した時刻は不明／今この場所で確認した／以前確認した」の選択を回答カードの中に追加。
- 今の観測を選んだ場合に限り、その回答時に `observed_at` を記録。以前の観測は利用者入力日時を変換。覚えていない・確認していない場合はNULL。
- `observed_time_source`：`UNKNOWN`／`AT_RESPONSE`／`RECALLED` を明示し、観測時刻が入力された根拠を区別。
- SQLは `docs/sql/my-rain-feedback-observation-time.sql` に**提案だけを保存**。追加カラム2件のみ、既存回答の時刻を過去の `updated_at` で埋めたりしない。旧回答は `observed_at=NULL`。
- 不明時刻でもフィードバック回答できる。時刻なしの回答を精度不良扱いしない。
- 単体テスト＋既存PostgreSQL RLS/HTTPテストへ観測日時の読書き・既存回答のNULL保持を追加。現行の配信/Push処理は変更なし。

## 実装上の留意点
- 「今」は現地で実際に目視した場合だけ。過去の天気を推測して今の日時を付けない。
- `RECALLED` の入力はiPhoneの現地時間（日時ローカル入力）。30日以上過去、未来時刻はクライアントで拒否。
- 旧回答を単に変更する場合は既存の観測時刻と情報源を維持し、勝手に「今」に変えない。
- **いまの回答時刻や観測時刻が得られても、過去の通知には予測対象時刻が保存されていない。通知タイミングや的中率の自動判定には使わない。**
- 現在のSupabase Freeプランでは隔離プロジェクトの追加不可。GitHub CIの使い捨てDBで先に検証。

## 本番反映ゲート
Issue #70で機能の受入れ判断、本番DBに新カラムを追加する**別途明示の承認**、現在のテーブル/権限の照合、移行SQL適用後の実iPhone検証が必要。
第1段階は本番で動作確認済み。新機能の準備が整うまでそれを維持し、PR #71はDraft。
Issue #64の運用障害セーフティネットは引き続き別管理。

## 追補：回答変更で観測時刻を改ざんしない（2026-10-22）
- 保存済みの天気回答だけを修正するとき、既存の `observed_at`/`observed_time_source` を維持する。新たな観測があったと勝手に仮定して「今」を入れ直さない。
- 利用者が観測日時を明示的に変える場合、過去の日時を入力欄へ復元してから編集可能。時刻を消すのも「覚えていない・確認していない」を**本人が選んだ場合のみ**。
- `tests/feedbackObservationTime.test.ts` に保持・明示変更・入力復元テストを追加。
- 最新のGitHub Actions run `39699682179` は **verify / phase1-feedback-http / phase1-feedback-rls 全件success**。ただし本番DBカラムは**未追加**、PR #71 Draftのまま。本番iPhoneテストや展開を許可なく行わない。

## 2026-10-22 10時台：回答の観測時刻カバー率表示
- 通知の見張り地点で絞り込んだ回答について「🕒 実際の確認時刻が記録された回答：N件／時刻不明：M件」を表示するようにした。**回答した人の記録の充実度であり、予測的中率ではない**。
- `summarizeObservationTimeCoverage` は閲覧している通知のIDだけ数え、旧回答で`observed_at`未登録なら未登録として扱う。回答UNKNOWNは時間が正しく記録されていれば観測時刻ありにカウントし、天気の正解/不正解には使わない。
- 認証セッションが無い場合や履歴取得に失敗した場合には、過去に読み込んだ観測時刻を画面上に残さないようにした。
- `tests/feedbackObservationTime.test.ts` に旧回答・地点フィルターに関する検証を追加。
- HEAD `b8add877` のGitHub Actions run `39703807411` は **verify / phase1-feedback-rls / phase1-feedback-http 全て成功**。
- 依然として **PR #71はDraft、本番DB移行は未適用、本番アプリは未変更**。iPhone実機の新しい時刻入力テストは未実施。先にDB移行内容と操作を確認してから公開する。

## 本番DBへの移行前・読み取り専用照合（2026-10-22）
- `EmergencyAlert` 本番 `public.rain_notification_feedback` の現行カラムは `notification_id` / `owner_id` / `actual_weather` / `updated_at` の4つ。**観測日時の2カラムはまだ存在しない**。
- 本番には回答が2件保存されており、RLSが有効。既存のPK・FK・回答値CHECKも存在。照合は `SELECT` のみ。**本番DBには一切書き込んでいない**。
- 提案移行 `docs/sql/my-rain-feedback-observation-time.sql` は2カラム追加と時刻/根拠の整合性制約。既存回答は `observed_at=NULL`, `observed_time_source='UNKNOWN'` になり、既存の回答を過去の`updated_at`から推測しない。
- 本番公開では `SQL移行 → PostgREST列反映 → 新しい画面のデプロイ → iPhoneで記録/再読込` の順番が必須。移行前に新画面だけ公開すると `loadPhase2ObservationTimes` が列不存在で失敗し、回答欄が使えなくなる。**PR #71 をそのまま先にmainマージしないこと**。
- 将来 `observed_at` が保存されたら、通知時刻との差だけを表示する。古い通知には予測対象時刻がないので、これは的中率・遅延判定ではない。
- 本番DB変更はこの照合では承認されていない。明示承認後に本番移行を実行すること。

## 観測と通知の差分表示（開発ブランチのみ）
- 新しく `observationVsDeliveryLabel` を用意。例えば通知25分後の観測なら「通知から25分後に確認」、通知より5分前なら「通知より5分前に確認」。観測時刻不明なら表示しない。
- 本人がいつ確認したかを表示し、**通知が遅かった/早かったとはまだ評価しない**。

## iPhone本番導入時の実機確認手順（まだ実行していない）
1. 本番スキーマの移行前・移行後を記録し、2件の既存回答が `observed_at = NULL`・`observed_time_source = UNKNOWN` で保持されていることを確認する。移行を新画面の公開より先に行う。
2. iPhoneで既存回答を開き、前の「降った」「止んだ」などの回答は保持され、確認時刻だけ「未登録」であることを確認する。
3. 過去の実観測なら「以前に確認した」を選び、実際に見た日時を指定して **「この回答の確認時刻だけ保存」** を押す。今現在その登録地点を目視している場合だけ「今」を選ぶ。
4. ホーム画面PWAを再起動し、観測時刻／通知からの時間差／確認時刻あり件数が復元されることを確認する。更新された `updated_at` を観測日時とは扱わない。
5. 回答文言だけ変更した際に観測日時が維持されること、観測日時を明示的に消したときは未登録になること、他の見張り地点の観測日時が混ざらないことを確認する。
6. 未来時刻と空の手入力は保存拒否、通知履歴が見えないセッションでは観測時刻が表示されないことを確認する。
7. 通常のPushが以前どおり届くことを確認する（Push workerの変更なし）。

## 実装中に検出したiPhone向け入力の不具合と修正
- 過去の観測時刻が入力欄に復元されていても、利用者が入力欄を**明示的に消した**場合は空欄として保存拒否する。「一度も編集されていない場合」と「本人が消した場合」を分ける。
- 旧実装は両方を空文字列で渡していたため、意図した消去を勝手に過去時刻へ戻す恐れがあった。`undefined` = 変更なし、`""` = 本人が空欄にした、として区別した。
- `tests/feedbackObservationTime.test.ts` に両ケースを追加した。

**注意:** この準備は本番DBの変更や本番デプロイの承認ではない。PR #71はDraftを維持。

## 2026-10-22 本番移行前の追加点検

- 現在の本番は旧Phase1回答2件と4カラム。次の手順は利用者の明示許可を得てから、もう一度読み取り専用で現況を照合し、DB移行後に新UIを公開すること。
- **この文書の更新、テスト成功や「りょ」だけを本番DB移行への一括許可と解釈しない。PR #71はDraft維持。本番書込みは保留。**
```
